# Ethiopian Synaxarium: Genbot (Mois de genbot)

**Author**: Anonymous (Ethiopian Synaxarion)  
**Series**: PO Tome 47  
**Columns**: pp. 200–360  

[p. 200] {#po.47.p200}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። ወርኃ ፡ ግንቦት ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ መዓልቱ ፡ ፲ወ፪ሰዓት ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ይትዌሰክ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. The month of Genbot (is) blessed. Its (first) day (is) fourteen hours, then (the days) increase.

### READING FOR THE FIRST OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ፳ለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ልደታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ' ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ንጽሕት" ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ባቲ ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ። ወኮነ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ክቡር ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ኢዮአቄም ፡ ወኢወለደ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ግሙራ ። ወኮነ ፡ የሐዝን ፡ በልቡ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ ያብእ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀጢአ ፤ ወልድ ፡ 'እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሕጎሙ ፡ ለካህናት" ፡ ኢያበውኡ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ለመካናት ። ወካዕበ ፡ ጸድቅት ፡ ሐና ፡ እማ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ተሐዝን ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀጢአ ፤ ወልድ" ። ወነጸረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ሕሊናሆሙ ፡ 'ወሠናይ ፡ ሑረቶሙ" ፡ ወሰምረ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ስሙ ፡ በኪነ ፡ ጥበቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ እምዘርኦሙ!! ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ኢዮአቄም" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወይጴጹሊ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ መዓልት" ፡ ወአስተርአዮ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዜነዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ 'እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይሁቦ”" ፡ ዘርአ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ እምኔሁ ፣ መድኃኒተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ወወረደ ፡ እምደብር ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የአምን ፡ በጥቡዕ ፡ ልብ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘሰምዓ ፡ እመልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወነገራ ፡ ለብእሲቱ ፡ 'ለዛቲ ፡ ራእይ” ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ወአእኩተቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአምነት ፡ ከመ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ እሙን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወበፅአት”' ፡ ብፅአተ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዘትወልዶ፤' ፡ ትረስዮ ፡ 'ላእኮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር” ፡ ወውስተ” ፡ ቤቱ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ፀንሰት ፡ ወወለደታ ፡ ለዛቲ ፤ ቅድስት ፤ ወሰመየታ” ፣ ማርያም ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሃ ፡ እግዝእት ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ትርጓሜሃ ፡ ጸጋ" ። 'በአማን ፡ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ” ፡ እግዝእተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወንግሥተ”ዖ ፡ ኩሎን ፡ አንስት” ፡ ወባቲ” ፡ ረከብነ ፡ ጸጋ” ። እግዚአብሔር ፡
On this day, there is the birth of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, the pure mother of God, through whom took place the salvation of the human race. Her father was the honorable old man 'Iyo'aqëm (= Joachim); he had not begotten any child. He was greatly distressed in his heart, for he could not offer sacrifice because of the lack of children: it was the custom of the priests not to offer sacrifice for the sterile (people). The just Hannä, her (= Mary's) mother, was also greatly distressed because of the lack of children. God considered their thought and their beautiful conduct, and he willed—blessed (be) his name!—by the operation of his wisdom that salvation should come from their offspring. While 'Iyo'aqëm was standing in the mountain and praying—until the completion of forty days—the angel of God appeared to him and announced to him that God would give him an offspring from whom would come the salvation of the whole world. (Iyo'aqëm) descended from the mountain, believing with a resolute heart in what he had heard from the angel of God. He told his wife this vision that he had seen; she gave thanks to God, believed that his word was truthful, and made a vow to God to make the child she would give birth to the servant of God and (that he/she be) in the house of the latter all the days of his/her life. After that, she conceived and gave birth to this saint; she called her Märyäm (= Mary), which, translated, (means) "lady." Its translation (is) also "grace." She is indeed truly the lady of the whole world and the queen of all women, and it is through her that we have found grace. May God have mercy on us through her prayer and may her intercession and her blessing be with us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 2ND OF GENBOT
ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ወትንብልናሃ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፪ለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ጻድቅ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ' ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጸድቀ ፡ ወጌቴረ ፡፤ በመዋዕሊሁ ። ወኮነ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ በውስተ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died 'Iyob (= Job) the just. This saint 'Iyob was just and virtuous during his days. God is witness regarding him in his holy Scriptures


[p. 202] {#po.47.p202}
መጻጸሕፍቲሁ ፡ ቅዱሳት" ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ዘከማሁ ። ወቀንአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዲያብሎስ" ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ ወኀሠሠ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ ክሂለ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ንዋዩ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ክሂለ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የአምር ፡ ትዕግሥቶ ፡ ለኢየብ ፡ ጻድቅ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ምሳሌ ፡ ወአርእአያ ፡ ለእለ ፣ ይመጽኡ ፣ እምድኅሬሁ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ በመልእክቱ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሰማዕክሙ ፡ ትዕግሥቶ ፡ ለኢዮብ" ፡ ወርኢክሙ ፡ ዘገብረ‹ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ' ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ጠፍአ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ንዋዩ ፡ ወእንስሳሁ ፡ ወውሉዱ ፡ ወአዋልዲሁ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ዓዲ ፤ ሥጋሁኒ ። እስመ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ ዘበጦ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ሥጋሁ ፥ በዝብጠተ ፡ደዊዌ ፡ ቀዮስል ፡ እምርእሱ ፡ እስከ ፡ እገሪሁ ። ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ደዌ" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትዔገሥ ፤ ወያአኩቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢያንጐረጉጐር" ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ወኢሀፀረፈ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ፡ አላ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረገማ ፡ ለዕለት ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ ባቲ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጠፍአ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ንዋዩ ፡ ወእንስሳሁ ፡ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ወለይኩን ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስከ ፡ 'ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም" ። ወነበረ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ጓዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ግዱፍ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ወግር"፤ ። ወይፈደፍድ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እምደዌሁ ፡ ተግሣጾሙ ፡ ለአርካኒሁ ፡ ዞወለብእሲቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ አምከረቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽርፍ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ፡ ወኢተአዘዘ ፡ ወኢሰምዓ ፡ ምክራ ። ወተፈትነ ፡ ወተነጥፈ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይፈትንዎ ፡ ወይነጥፍዎ"' ፡ ለወርቅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እቶነ" ፡ እሳት ። ወተናገሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ደመና ፡ ወፈወሶ ፡ እምደዌሁ ። ወአመክዐበ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡
that no one during his days was just like him. The devil was jealous of him, addressed God, and asked (him) to give him power over him and over all his goods. God gave him power over him, for he knew the constancy of 'Iyob the just: this one would be an example and a model for those who would come after him, as Yä'qob (= James) the Apostle says in his epistle: "Behold, you have heard (about) the constancy of 'Iyob and have seen what God has done for him." Of this just man, in one day, perished all his goods, the herds, the sons, and the daughters—and not only that (but) also his body; indeed, Satan the Enemy struck him in his body with the blows of leprosy from the head to the feet. He remained in all this illness constant and praising God. He did not murmur a single day nor did he blaspheme against his creator, but he said only this, namely that he cursed the day when he was born. When all his goods and his herds perished, he said: "God gave, God (has) taken (back). So be it. Blessed (be) the name of God in the ages of ages." He remained thus in this misfortune thirty years lying on a mound. Stronger against him than the illness were the representations of his three friends and of his wife. This one indeed advised him to blaspheme against his Creator. He did not obey her nor did he listen to her advice. He was tested and purified as one tests and purifies gold in a furnace of fire. God spoke to him from the inside of a cloud and healed him of his illness. He (returned) to him double all his goods and gave him other sons. ('Iyob) lived in a beautiful old age, pleased God, and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ወወሀቦ ፡ ውሉዶ ፡ ካልኣነ ፡ ወሐይወ ፡ 'በርሥዕና ፡ ሠናይት" ፡ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፤ ጳዙሚስ'' ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ ኮነ ፤ ጽሙድ ፡ ወትሑት ፡ ወየዋህ ፡ ወመስተጋድል ፤ ወተአዛዚ ፡ ወፍጹም ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሠናይ” ። ወኮነ ፡ ይትኤዘዝ” ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ጳኩሚስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይትኤዘዝ” ፡ ለአምላክ ። ወበእንተዝ”ዖ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ያፈቅሮ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ጸጋ ፡ ወናዛዜዎ ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡ አኃው ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወአእምሮ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ረሰዮ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ አባ ፡ ጳዙሚስ ፡ ይገሥጾሙ ፡ ለአኃው ፡ ወይምሕሮሙ” ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ 'ወሬዛ ፡ ውእቱ” ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ እረፍቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ተሰይመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡፤ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ሱታፌ” ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባመ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ትሕትናሁ” ፡ ወይጐይይ ፡ እምክብረ ፡ ከንቱ ፡ ዝዓለም ፣፤ ወፈጸመ ፡ ግብሮ ፣ ወመልእክቶ” ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡
On this day also died the holy father Abba Téwodros (= Theodore), the disciple of the holy father Abba Päkuemis (= Pachomius). This saint was devout, humble, gentle, ascetic, obedient, and perfect in every beautiful action. He obeyed the holy Abba Päkuemis as he obeyed God. Because of that, the holy Abba Päkuemis loved him much. Grace was upon him and (he was) a consoler for all the brothers who (were) in the community; he had wisdom and science. Because of that, the holy Abba Päkuemis had him exhort the brothers and teach them while he was (still) an adolescent. After the death of Abba Päkuemis, this father Téwodros was named abbot of the community in the place of the holy Abba Päkuemis. This saint was very humble and fled the vain glory of this world. He accomplished his task and his office and went to God who loved him,


[p. 204] {#po.47.p204}
እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘአፍቀሮ ፡ እንዘ ፡፤ ይለብስ ፡ አክሊለ፤ ፡ ቅድስና ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤሲ ፡ ፳ወ፪ነፍስ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
being clothed with the crown of holiness. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us, amen. [On this day (also) twenty-four people became martyrs with Abba 'Ési. May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear 'Atnätëwos in the ages of ages.]

### READING FOR THE 3RD OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ሾለግንቦት ፡-ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ያሶን ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለድወ፪አርድእት ፡ እለ ፡፤ ኀረዮሙ ፡ እግዚእነ'፤ ። ወሰበከ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወተወክፈ ፥ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወመድኃኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወገብረ ፡ ታአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ። ወወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ኀይል ፡ ወጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ዛወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ስብከት ። ወኮነ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ተርሴስ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ዘአምነ ፡ "እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ተርሲስ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተለዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ለስብከት ። ወሖረ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ብዙኅ ። ወአኀዝዎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወሲላስ ፡ በውስተ" ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ተስሎንቄ ። ወሰሐብዎ ፡ ወአብጽሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ተስሎንቄ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አንበረ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወሜሞ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆጾሰ ፡ 'ላዕለ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ተርሲስ" ። ወዓቀበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኖ ፡ 'ለወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔርጎ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ፡ ወጊሩት ። 'ወሶበ ፡ ዓጽንዖሙ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወምግባራተ ፤ ሠናይ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ምዕራብ ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ በስብከተ ፡ ወንጌል" ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy apostle Yäson (= Jason). This saint was in the number of the seventy-two disciples that our Lord chose. He preached with the disciples, suffered the sufferings of our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, and performed many miracles and wonders. The power and the grace of the Holy Spirit the Paraclete descended upon him (who was) with the Apostles, the day of the feast of Pentecost, and he went in preaching. He was born in the city of Tarsës (= Tarsus) and he (is) the first of the people of the city of Tarsus who believed. After that, he followed the holy apostle Päwlos (= Paul) for the preaching. He went with him into many cities. They apprehended him with Päwlos and Siläs (= Silas) in the city of Taslongë (= Thessalonica); they dragged him and brought him to the governor of Taslongë. After that, Päwlos imposed his hands on him and consecrated him bishop of his city of Tarsus. He guarded the Church of the Son of God with a beautiful guard and (with) virtue. When he had strengthened them (= the inhabitants) in the right faith and had taught them all the rules of the Church and the beautiful actions, he went toward the cities of the west and dispensed there the preaching of the holy Gospel.
ቅዱስ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደሴት ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ከርኩራስ'' ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ በስብከተ ፡ ወንጌል" ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ በስሙ ፡ 'ለቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ" ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ዲያቆናት" ። አእመረ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አኀዞ ፡ ወሞቅሖ ። ወረከበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ሂፈያታዊያነ ፡ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ሃይማኖተ" ፡ እግዚእነ” ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወጸርተ ፡ ኅበ"' ፡ መኩንን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ገሃደ ። ወወደዮሙ” ፡ መኩንን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቴገናት ፡ ምሉኣተ ፡ ዝፍት” ፡ ወተይ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ” ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አውጽኦ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ያሶን፤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወኩጎነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወኢበጽሖ ፤ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እሙስና ። ወርእየቶ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እመስኮተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ወአምነት ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወአውፅአት ፡ አልባሲሃ ፡ ቀጠንተ ፡ ወሰርጓቲሃ” ፡ ወወሀበቶሙ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ወተአምነት” ፡ ከመ ፡
He arrived at an island by the name of Karkweräs (= Corcyra), dispensed there the preaching of the Gospel, and built there a church in the name of the holy apostle 'Estifänos (= Stephen), the archdeacon. The governor of this region learned of this, he apprehended him, and imprisoned him. (The saint) found in the prison seven robbers; he taught them the faith of our Lord Christ and baptized them with Christian baptism. They cried out to the governor saying: "We are Christians openly." The governor (had) them thrown into cauldrons full of pitch and sulfur, and they received the crown of martyrdom. After that, he brought the holy apostle Yäson out of the prison and made him undergo great torture, but no damage happened to this one. The daughter of the governor saw him


[p. 206] {#po.47.p206}
ይእቲ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ 'ወተአምን ፡ በአምላኩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኢያሶን” ። ወተምዐ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ” ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ” ፡ ወአጤሱ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ነደፋ” ፣ በአሕፃ ፡ ወመጠወት ፡ ነፍሳ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፤ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኢያሶን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደሴት ፣ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ሰማዕታት ። ወተጽዕነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ሰራዊቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፣ ወይኩንኖሙ ፡ በህየ ። ወአስጠሞ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወለኩሎሙ ፡እለ ፡ሀለዉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወአእኩቶ” ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኢያሶን ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ድኅነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ይሰብክ ፡ ወይሜህር ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ካልእ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ለቅዱስ”! ፡ ወለእለ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወመልአ ፡ ቴገነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ዝፍተ” ፡ ወሰምዐ ፡ ወአንደዱ ፡ እሳተ ፡ መትሕቴሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተመስወ ። ወወደይዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኢያሶን ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ወአድኃኖ ፡ እግዚአብሔር” ፡ ወኢበጽሖ ፡ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ እሙስና ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አምነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወሰብአ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ትአዛዛተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ። ወገብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፤ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡፤ ብዙኃተ ። ወአዕረፈ ፡ 'በርሥዕና ፡ ሠናይት” ፡ ወበገጽ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ።
From the window of her house, she believed in our Lord Christ, stripped off her precious garments and ornaments, gave them to the poor and the needy, and confessed that she was a Christian and believed in the God of the holy ‘lyäson. Her father became angry and ordered her to be thrown into prison, and it was done as he had ordered. She was locked up, then he had her pierced with arrows, and she surrendered her soul into the hand of God. After that, he sent the holy ‘lyäson to an island, with other martyrs with him. The governor embarked on a boat—his soldiers with him—to go to them and have them tortured there. God caused him to sink in the sea with all those who were with him. The holy ‘lyäson gave thanks to God. After that, this saint was saved and remained preaching and teaching for many years. Another governor was appointed, and he had the saint and those who were with him brought. He had a large cauldron filled with pitch and wax, and a fire was kept burning under it until the mixture had become liquid. They threw the holy ‘lyäson into it; God saved him, and no harm came to him. When the governor saw this, he believed in our Lord Christ, as did the people of his house and all the people of the city. The apostle baptized them with Christian baptism, taught them the precepts of the holy Gospel, and built churches for them. This apostle performed many miracles and wonders among them. He died in a beautiful old age and with a joyful face, and was pleasing to God. May his prayer and his blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen.
ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ” ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ዘእምሰብአ ፡ ቤልጌት” ፡ 'በተወክፎ ፡ ስምዕ” ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኩነንዎ ፣ በመጥወይ ፡ ወበመዋቅሕት ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ አክሊል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ነዋ ፡ አክሊልከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴየ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ኩነንዎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወወረውዎ ፡ ውስተ” ፡ እሳት ። ወፈጸመ ፡ ሩጸቶ ፡ ሠናየ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡
[On this day also died, receiving martyrdom, abbä Bsoy, who was of the people of Bëlgêt. They tortured this saint with a rack and with chains. Then the angel of God appeared to him, a crown in his hand, and said to him: "Do not fear, behold your crown is in my hand." After that, they tortured him greatly and threw him into the fire. He completed his beautiful career. May his holy blessing be with his dear ‘Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]
ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፤ ወክቡር ፡ አውሳብዮስ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ ሴም ፡ ሰማዕት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ ለፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also, there is the commemoration of the holy and glorious ‘Awsäbos (= Eusebius) the priest and of the holy Sem (= Shem) the martyr. May God have mercy on their dear ‘Atnätëwos for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካረ ፤ ዕረፍታ ፡ ለንግሥት ፡ ቡርክት ፡ ወለተ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እሙ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ 'ክቡር ፡ ወናግ” ፡ ሰገድ ። ትንብልናሀ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁራ ፡፤ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ወፍቅርታሂ ፡ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, there is the commemoration of the death of the blessed queen Walatta Märyäm, who is the mother of the glorious king Wanäg sagad. May her intercession be with her dear ‘Atnätëwos and also her dear Kotolikäwit for ever and ever, amen.]


[p. 208] {#po.47.p208}

### READING FOR THE 4TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ 8ለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ' ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምውሉደ ፤ መሃይምናን ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ዘደብረ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ" ፡ ዘእምቅድሜሁ ፡ ኅረይዎ”' ፡ 'ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ወማእምራን ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወነሥእዎ' ፡ በኩርህ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ኢይፈቅድ ፡ዘንተ ። አላ ፡ ሰአልዎ ፡ ስእለታተ” ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይኅድግ ፣ ካልእ ፡ ይሠየም ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መርዔት ፡ ወየኀሥሞሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ለኤሏስ ፡ ቆጾሳት ፡ ወለማእምራን ፡ ወለረበናት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይስእልዎ ፡ ፈርሃ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዮጊ ፡ ይከውንኑ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ" ፡ ሐለየ ፡ በእንተ ፣ መርዔቱ ፡ ሕሊና ፤ ውሱከ ፡ በትምህርታት'፤፤ ፡ ወአንብቦ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ሃይማኖት ፡ርትዕት ። ወሜሞሙ ፡ ለኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ወለካህናት ፡ ማእምራን ። ወሀሎ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ቀስጥንጥንያ ፡ ዜኖን" ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ወመሃይምን ፡ ወይፈርሆ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወረድኦ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወሰፍሐ ፤ እዴሁ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አህጉር" ፡ ወተሰፍሐ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፤ ግብጽ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዜኖን” ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፤ አሕማራተ ፡ ምሉኣተ ፡ ስርናይ ፡ ወወይን ፡ ወዘይት ፡ ወንዋይ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡፣ በእንተ ፡ ሕንጸተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ወአድባራት ፡ ወዘይፈቅዱ ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ" ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በህድአት ፡ ወሰላም ። ወሠምረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወበትምህርቱ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሐወጾ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በሕማም ፡ ንስቲት ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ነበረ"! ፤ በሲመቱ ፡ ፳ዓመተ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father abbä Yohannes (= John), patriarch of the city of Alexandria. This father was of the children of the believers among the people of the city of Alexandria. He became a monk from his childhood in the desert of ‘Asgêtes (= Scetis), that of the monastery of the holy abbä Magäres (= Macarius). When abbä ‘Atnäsyos (= Athanasius), who was patriarch before him, died, the bishops, the doctors, and all the people chose this father and took him against his will: he himself did not want this. But they begged him with many prayers not to let another be appointed at the head of the flock: it would be harmful to him. When he saw the bishops, the doctors, and the elders begging him, he feared them and said: "Perhaps this is happening by the will of our Lord Jesus Christ." They consecrated him patriarch. When he was seated on the throne of Märgos (= Mark), the evangelist, he applied redoubled care to his flock with teachings and the reading of the Scriptures, and he strengthened it in the right faith. He consecrated learned bishops and priests. In those days, the emperor in the city of Questentenyä (= Constantinople) was Zënon (= Zeno) the just and believing; he feared God. He helped this saint and extended his hand over the lands; the right faith spread throughout the land of Egypt. In those days, the emperor Zënon sent to the desert of ‘Asgêtes, that of the monastery of the holy abbä Magäres, boats full of wheat, wine, oil, and many goods for the construction of churches and monasteries and for what they needed. All the days of this father passed in tranquility and peace. God was benevolent toward the people through the prayer of this father and through his teaching. Then God visited this father with a small illness, and he died in peace after remaining in his office for eight years. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ ሶሲማ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ኖዳ ፡ አግብርቲሁ ፡ ለፊቅጦር ። "ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ"' ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፤ ፍቁሮሙ" ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also became martyrs Sosimä (= Zosimus?) and abbä Nodä, the servants of Figtor (= Victor). May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear ‘Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 5TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ፳ለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኪልቅዩ ፡ ካህን ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ፪እምነቢያት ፡ ዓቢያን፤' ። ዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died ‘Érmeyäs (= Jeremiah), the just prophet, son of Kilgeyu (= Hilkiah) the priest, who is the second of the great prophets. This just man prophesied


[p. 210] {#po.47.p210}
ለኢዮስያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አሞጽ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወኢዮአቄም ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሴዴቅያስ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ" ፡ ነበበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀደስኩከ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ትጻእ ፡ እምከርሠ ፡ እምከ ፡ ወረሰይኩከ ፡ ነቢየ ፡ ለአሕዛብ! ። ወገሠጾሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡፤ እስራኤል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኀደጉ ፡ አምልኮ ፥፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወመነኑ ፡ ሕጎ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ተዓቀቡ ፡ ወተነሥሔሑ”" ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይምጻጳእ ፡ መዓቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ። ወአጠየቆሙ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢተመየጥክሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወእመአኮ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ 'ያመጽእ ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ፡ ናቡክድናጸጾር ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ከላዳዊያን ፡ ወይዴውወክሙ ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ኮነ ፡ ወፄወዎሙ ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ንብረቶሙ ፡፣ ለሕዝብ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ፄዋዌ ፡ ድዓመተ ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ "ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወመድኃኒነ' ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ቋብሩር ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ አስቆሮታዊ ፡ ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጸአተ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ እምጽዮን ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምግባራት“ ፡ ብዙኃት ። ወፈቀዱ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ጊዜ ፤ ወቀሠፍዎ ፡ ወሞቅሕዎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይጴሊ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይስእል ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢትጸሊ ፡ ወኢታስተብቀዕ ፡ ኅቤየ"! ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወኢትተንብል'! ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ እስመ” ፡ ኢይሰምዕ ፡ ስእለተከ ፤ በእንቲአሆሙ ። 'ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እምፄዋዌ ፡ ባቢሎን" ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወበጸሎቱ” ፡ አጥፍኦሙ”" ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአራዊት ፡ ወአናብስት" ፡ እለ ፣ ውስተ ፡ አፍላገ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፥ የኀስምዎሙ!፤ ፡ ለግብጻዊያን ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡፤ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ግብጻዊያን ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በዓለ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ።
in the days of ‘lyosyäs (= Josiah) son of ‘Amos (= Amon), king of Yehudä (= Judah), and of ‘lyo’agëm (= Jehoiakim) son of Sédëqyäs (= Zedekiah). God spoke concerning this just man and said: "Before I formed you in the womb I knew you, and before you were born I consecrated you; I appointed you a prophet to the nations." This prophet rebuked the children of Israel because they had abandoned the worship of God and despised his law. He said to them: "Take heed and repent, lest the wrath of God come upon you." He gave them a sign and said to them: "If you do not return to God, he will bring against you Näbukednäsor (= Nebuchadnezzar), the king of the Chaldeans, and he will take you into captivity." Thus it happened, and he took them into captivity. He prophesied about the stay of the Jews in captivity for seventy years. He prophesied about the coming of our Lord and Savior, about his sufferings, and about the thirty pieces of silver that Yehudä (= Judas) Iscariot received. He prophesied that the law of the Gospel would go forth from Seyon (= Zion). He prophesied about many things. The Jews wanted to kill him many times. They scourged him and imprisoned him many times. He prayed continually to God and begged him on their behalf. God said to him: "Do not pray or implore me for this people, do not intercede for them, for I will not listen to your supplication concerning them." After the people had returned from the captivity of Bäbilon (= Babylon), ‘Érmeyäs went to the land of Egypt, and through his prayer, God destroyed the wild beasts and the lions that were in the Torrent of Egypt, which were causing harm to the Egyptians. When the days of his prophecy were completed and it had pleased God, he died in peace in the city of Alexandria. The Egyptians began to celebrate a feast for him on the same day as this day. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እልፍዮስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also, there is the commemoration of the holy Yä’qob (= James) the Apostle, son of ‘Elfeyos (= Alphaeus). May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 6TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ሄለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ' ፡ አባ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ድፍራ" ፡ እምደቡበ ፣ ግብጽ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በራእየ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወአንቅሖ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ጡው ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሣእ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትአምኖሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ወለእሙ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ወበከዩ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became a martyr abbä Yeshäq (= Isaac), who was from the city of Defrä (= Tiphré) in the north of Egypt. The angel of God appeared to this saint in a vision by night, awakened him, and said to him: "Go to the city of Tuw (= Touah) to receive the crown of martyrdom." He arose to say goodbye to his father

and to his mother before going away. They wept over him and did not let him go until the angel of God came a second time, made him leave his city, and brought him to the city of Tuw. When he arrived, he found the governor at the baths. When the governor came out, the saint cried out before him, saying: "I am a Christian openly; I believe in my Lord Christ." The governor ordered a soldier to take the saint with him and guard him until he himself had returned from the city of Nagiyos (= Nikiou). While the saint abbä Yeshaq was passing with the soldier, a blind man who was sitting by the road begged this saint, saying: "Have mercy on me, saint of God, and heal my eyes." The saint abbä Yeshaq begged our Lord Jesus Christ for the blind man, and his eyes were opened immediately. When the soldier saw this miracle, he believed in our Lord Jesus Christ and became a Christian. When the governor returned, the soldier confessed our Lord Christ before him and received the crown of martyrdom. After that, the governor subjected this saint to great torture and sent him to the city of Behnesä (= Behnesah). There they subjected him to various tortures. While he was on a boat, the saint asked for a cup of water to be given to him. One of the sailors gave him a cup of water—he was blind in one eye. The saint poured some of this water on him, and the blind eye opened like the other. When the people of the city of Behnesa saw the many tortures with which they had tortured the saint abbä Yeshaq and the great miracles he performed, they rose up against the governor and said to him: "Either you send him away or you kill him." Then the governor ordered his head to be cut off; they cut off his holy head with the sword, and he received the crown of life. There were believers there; they carried the body of the saint abbä Yeshaq on a cart, oxen pulled it and brought it to the city of Defra. As they could not find a boat to take it across to the other side of the river, the oxen crossed the river carrying the body of the saint abbä Yeshaq. The believers brought it to his house, destroyed his house, built a church for him in his name, and deposited his body there. Many miracles and wonders occurred there. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

On this day also died the holy and glorious father abbä Magäres (= Macarius), the Alexandrian priest. This saint lived in the time of the holy father abbä Magäres the Great, and he was abbot of the mountain of ‘Asgêtes (= Scetis). He practiced many virtues. It is said of him that a mosquito bit him: he killed it, repented, and rebuked himself for having killed it. He went down into a valley that was in the desert and exposed his body to the mosquitoes; he remained there six months until the mosquitoes had ulcerated his body and he had become like a sick man whose body is ulcerated. After the six months, he returned

to his cell, and no one recognized that he was abbä Magäres. Once, he stood praying for five days and five nights—his heart was in heaven—so much so that the demons grew heated and said: "This labor and this trial are greater than all the exploits he has done." Once, he wanted to see the garden of the time of the giants. He entered the desert and remained walking for ten days. He had reeds that he placed on the path so that they would be markers for him so that, when he returned, he would not lose his way. When he was about to leave this place, he was weary and lay down to rest a little. As for Satan, he pulled up the reeds, tied them, and placed them near the head of the sleeping saint. When abbä Magäres awoke from his sleep, he saw the reeds and was astonished. He heard a voice saying to him: "Magäres, if you have faith, do not put your hope in reeds, but believe that it is the pillar of cloud that guided the children of Israel in the desert that will guide you." When the saint had seen the garden, he turned back and became thirsty on the way. God sent him a buffalo, he drank of its milk until he was quenched, and returned to his cell. Once, a hyena came to him and began to pull his clothes; he followed her to her cave. She brought out her three cubs for him; he noticed that their eyes were blind and admired the intelligence of the animal. Then he took them in his hands, groaned, and spat saliva on their eyes. Then he traced on them with his fingers the sign of the life-giving Cross. They were healed immediately, ran behind their mother, suckled her milk, and followed her until she had entered her cave. After that, the hyena returned to the saint and brought him a sheepskin; it remained near him—he would lie on it—until the moment of his death.

Once, he changed his clothes, dressed in the image of a layman, and went to the monastery of the holy abbä Päkuemis (= Pachomius). He stood for the forty days of the Great Fast; he did not eat, did not drink, nor did he sit, but he wove palm branches while standing. The monks said to abbä Päkuemis: "Send this man away from us, for he has no body." Abbä Päkuemis said to them: "Wait for me until I have asked God to reveal to me what concerns him." When he had questioned God about him, it appeared to him that it was abbä Magäres, the Alexandrian priest. Then the holy abbä Päkuemis went—all the monks with him—to him; they greeted him, received his blessing, rejoiced at his coming, and brought him into the sanctuary of the church with them. When they saw the exercises of this saint abbä Magäres, those who prided themselves on their actions in the monastery of the holy abbä Päkuemis became humble. city of Alexandria, the patriarch abbä Timotéwos (= Timothy) dispatched to him and asked him to come to him to the city of Alexandria and to pray to God with him so that rain might fall and the locusts might perish. The saint arose and went with them to the city of Alexandria; the people welcomed him with great joy. He prayed in petto, in secret, and a great rain fell. It remained falling like that for two days and two nights, so much so that the people thought the earth was going to perish because of the abundance of the rain. The saint abbä Magäres said to the patriarch: "Why did you make me come here?" The people answered him and said to him: "We made you come, our father, so that you might pray to God so that he would remove the rain from us and that we might not all perish." Immediately Magäres prayed, the rain ceased, and there was a great clap of thunder in the sky by the power of God. This father performed many great and prodigious actions; he healed many people who had unclean spirits. When he performed a good deed and people had knowledge of the good deed he had done, he himself did not count it as a good deed that he had done. When he heard that a man had done a good deed, he did not sleep until he had acted in accordance with that good deed. When he had completed his life in a beautiful old age, he died in peace. He remained sixty years without ever spitting saliva on the ground because of the honor due to the flesh and blood of our Lord Jesus Christ. All the days of his life were one hundred years. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ በብኑዳ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ በንደራ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በንድላኦን ፣፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኤስድሮስ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also became a martyr the saint abbä Babnudä (= Paphnuce), who was from the city of Bandarä (= Bandarah). May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen. [On this day also became a martyr Bandelä’on (= Pantaleon), the father of the saint ‘Ésderos (= Isidore). May his holy blessing be with his dear ‘Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ፅዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አሞን ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ዘዐበየ ፡ ሚመተ ፡ ጵጵስና ፡እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ይጌይሰኒ ፡ ለእመ ፡ አጥረይኩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ጸሎቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቅርትሂ ፡ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ዲላጊ!' ፡ ወአርባዕቱ ፡ ደቂቃ ፡ እለ ፡ ስሞሙ ፡ ሱርስ ፡ ወኅርማን ፡ ያኑፋ ፡ ወስንጣንያ ። ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ጽንዕተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ ሀገረ ፡
[On this day also died ‘Amon (= Ammon) the just, who refused the episcopal office, saying: "It is better for me to possess the monastic practice." May his prayer be with his dear ‘Atnätéwos and with his dear Kotolikäwit for ever and ever, amen.] [On this day also became martyrs the saint Dilägi (= Pelagia) and her four sons whose names are Sures, Hermän, Yänufa, and Sentänyä. This saint was firm in the faith in our Lord Christ—glory to him! When

እንስና ፣፡ ተቀበለቶ ፣፤ ቅድመ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትነድእ ፤ ደቂቃ ፣ ወቆመት” ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ኦአርያኖስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ አንሰ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ መሲሓዊት ፡ መሃይምንት ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ዘፈጠረ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘውስቴታ ። ወደቂቃኒ ፡ ይኬልሑ ፡ ከማሀ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ክር ስቲያን ፡ ገሃደ” ፡ መሲሓዊያን"፤ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ መኩንን ፡ መልዐ ፡ መዐተ ፡ ወመተረሞ ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ። ወፈጸሙ ፡ ስምዖፆሙ ፡ ወተመጠዉ ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
‘Aryänos (= Arianus) arrived in the city of ‘Ensenä (= Antinoe), she welcomed him first by pushing her children before her; she stood before him and said to him: "‘Aryänos the governor, I am a Christian believing in our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him!—who created the heaven and the earth, the sea, and everything that is in them." Her children also cried out the same, saying: "We are Christians openly." Upon hearing this, the governor was filled with anger and had their heads cut off with the sword. They completed their martyrdom, and angels received their souls near our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him! May their holy blessing be with their dear ‘Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ” ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ደናስዮስ ፡ ዘፈጸመ ፤ ገድሎ” ፡ በአፈ ፤ መጥባሕት ፤ ወሳምራዊት ። 'ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ” ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ” ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also died the holy father Danäsyos (= Dionysius), who completed his life under the edge of the knife, as well as the Samaritan woman. May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear ‘Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ አዕፅረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ በዐልተ ፡ ንጽሕ ፤ ወቅድስና ፤ ተኃራሚት ፡ ድንግልት ፡ ሰሎሜ ። ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ወረብ ። ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ፈራህያነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሐፀንዋ ፡ ለሠናይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀት ፡ ሐፀያ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወአዕተዋ ፡ በሥርዓተ ፡ ከብካብ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢትፈቅድ ፡ ለሊሃ ። ወፈቀደ ፡ ይቅረብ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ከልኦ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ አምላካዊ ፡ ወቀሠፎ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወኢክህለ ፡ ቀሪበ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ኀብአት ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ወሖረት ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወአምሠጠት ፡ በኀይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወነበረት ፡ እንዘ ፤ ታሜክር ፡ ወተአውድ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወትስእል ፡ መዐልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምርሓ”' ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፍኖተ ፡፤ ጽድቅ ። ወሰምዓ ፡ ስእለታ ፡ ወበፈቃዱ ፡ ለአምላክ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሊባኖስ ፡ ወለብሰት ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ሕገ ፡ መላእክት”ዎ ፡ በእደ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ከማ ። ተጋደለት ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዘያደነግፅ ፡ አልባበ ፡ ወሖረት ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ አበው ፡ ትሩፋን” ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡ ገድል ፡ ውዱስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ገብረት ፡ ተአምራተ” ፣ ብዙኃ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ በመጽሐፈ” ፡ ገድላ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ አመ ፡ ለግንቦት ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁራ ፡
On this day also died the holy lady of purity and holiness, the chaste and virgin Salomë (= Salome). This saint was from the city of Warab. Her parents feared God and raised her for good. When she had grown up, a man took her as a fiancée and brought her to marriage, although she herself did not want it. He wanted to know her, but immediately the divine power prevented him, struck him in his flesh, and he could not know her. While she was in this situation, she hid, left by night, and escaped by the power of God. She remained studying and frequenting all the saints and begging day and night in fasting and prayer so that God would show her the right way. God heard her supplication, and by the divine will, she arrived at Dabra Libänos and put on the monastic garment—which is the law of the angels—by the hand of Yohannes (= John) Kamä. She waged many spiritual combats that terrify the hearts and followed the way of the virtuous fathers in fasting, in prayer, and in every praiseworthy spiritual combat, so much so that she performed many miracles, as it is written in the book of her life. Then she died on the sixth of Genbot. May her prayer and her blessing be with her dear ‘Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.]
አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ክቡር ፡ አርሰይ ፤ መትረያድስ ፡ አባ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ጳጳስ ፤ አዕማደ ፡ ቤተ ፡፤ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወዲዮስቆሮስ ፤ መምህረ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወሕግ ፡ ዮልዮስኒ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ዘተሳየጠ”፤ ፡ ለመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ በወርቀ ፡ ደሙ ፡ ቅድስት ። በረከተ ፡ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡፤ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also died the illustrious father, the archimandrite abbä Sem’on (= Simon), the metropolitan pillar of the Church, Diyosqoros (= Dioscorus), the doctor of the law and of the faith, and Yolyos (= Julius) the martyr, the spiritual combatant who bought the kingdom of heaven with the gold of his holy blood. May the blessing of their prayer be with their dear ‘Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 7TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ሄለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ፤ ፡ ሐዋርያዊ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡፳። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምውሉደ" ፡ አረማዊያን ። ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ እለ ፡ ይትሜሀሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መምህር ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ለውሉዶ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡እንዘ ፡ ይገብሩ ፤ ሥርዐተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣፤ ወይትዋነዩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ። ወረሰዩ ፡ ቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ ቀሳውስተ ፡ ወቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ ዲያቆናተ ፡ ወለካልኡ" ፡ ረሰይዎ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሰ ። ወሰአሎሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ለውሉዶ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሳተፍ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ወከልእዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ አረማዊ ፡ ወንሕነሰ ፡ ኢንደመር ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አነ ፡ እከውን ፡ ክርስቲያናዌ ፡ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ቦቱ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ ትከውን ፡ 'ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ" ። ወእምዝ ፡ ረሰዩ ፡ መትሕቴሁ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ መንበር ፡ ወአኀዙ ፡ ይስግዱ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ኅለፈ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፣ ለውሉድዶ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትዋነዩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ሀለዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰየም ፡ ሚመተ ፡ ልዕልተ ፡ ወክብርተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፣ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ" ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወወሀቡ ፡ ዙሎ ፡፤ ንዋዮሙ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ በኀበ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ። 'ወመሀሮ ፡ አባ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትምህርታተ ፡ ወሕገ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፍቁረ ፡ ወሜሞ ፡ ዲያቆነ ፡ ወተመክአበ ፡ ላዕሌሁ” ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ 7፣
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father abbä ‘Atnäsyos (= Athanasius) the Apostolic, patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarch fathers, he is the twentieth. This father was of the children of the pagans. While he was sitting with the small children who were being instructed by a master, he saw the children of the Christians reproducing the institutions of the Church and playing among themselves. They declared some of them priests and some of them deacons, and they declared another bishop. This father asked the children of the Christians to join them in what they were doing. They prevented him and said to him: "You are a pagan, we do not mix with you." He said to them: "I will become a Christian." They rejoiced at it and said to him: "You will be patriarch." Then they placed a kind of throne under him and began to prostrate themselves before him. At that moment, the father abbä ‘Eleskenderos (= Alexander) the patriarch passed by. When he saw the children of the Christians playing among themselves, this father said to those who were with him: "This child will be appointed to a high and glorious office." When the father of this father died, he himself came with his mother to this father abbä ‘Eleskenderos the patriarch, and he baptized him with Christian baptism. They gave all their goods to the poor and the needy and remained with the father abbä ‘Eleskenderos. Abbä ‘Eleskenderos taught this father abbä ‘Atnäsyos all the sciences and the law of the Church and made him a beloved son. He ordained him deacon, and the Holy Spirit was redoubled upon him. When abbä ‘Eleskenderos died, they appointed this father abbä ‘Atnäsyos patriarch of the city of Alexandria.
እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ሜምዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ'፤ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ህየንቴሁ” ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አርዮሳዌ ፡ ወበዝ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ አርዮስ ። ወሰደዶ ፡፣ ቁቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ” ፡ አትናስዮስ” ፡ እምሚመቱ ፡ ወአንበረ ፡ ሀየንቴሁ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ገርጊዮስ" ። ወነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ስደት ፡ ሄዓመተ ፡ ርሑቀ ፡ እመንበረ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ምዕራብ ። ወሀሎ ፡ በሀየ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ ወይትገብኡ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወበውስቴታ" ፡ ለይእቲ'' ፡ ቤተ ፤ ጣዖታት ፡ ምግባራት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ዘሰይጣናት ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ይስእሎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እስከ ፡ አንሐላ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ጣዖታት ። ወሜጦሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ" ፡ ወአብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሜጦ ፡፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ ለገርጊዮስ' ፡ አርዮሳዊ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ጂዓመተ ። ወሖሩ ፡ አርዮሳዊያን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአስተዋደይዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በሐሰት ። ወፈነወ ፡
When Quastantinos (= Constantine) the just emperor died, his son Quastantinos (= Constantius) reigned in his place; he was an Arian, and the people of Arius multiplied. Quastantinos the emperor drove this father abbä ‘Atnäsyos from his office and installed in his place a man by the name of Gargiyos (= George). This father remained in exile for six years, far from his patriarchal seat, in the land of the West. There was a temple of idols there; many people gathered there. In this temple of idols, many satanic actions were performed. This saint begged our Lord Christ until he had destroyed this temple of idols. He converted all the people and brought them into the knowledge of God. After that, God brought him back to the city of Alexandria and drove out Gargiyos the Arian. This father remained on his seat for seven years. The Arians went to the emperor and brought a false accusation against this father.


[p. 222] {#po.47.p222}
ንጉሥ ፡ አሐደ ፡ መስፍነ ፡ ወአኀዞ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወሞቅሖ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፤ ሮሜ ፤ ወሊቀ” ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፣ አንጾኪያ ፡፤ ወፈነወ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ወአድኅኖሙ” ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጫመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ወልዶ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ርቱዓ ፣ ሃይማኖት ። ወሜጦ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ፳ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ በህድአት ፡ ወሰላም ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፤ ቅስጦስ” ፡ ወነግሠ ፣ እምድኅሬሁ ፡፤ ሉልያኖስ ፤ ከሐዲ” ፡ ወኀሠሦ ፡ ለዝንቱ” ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ” ፡ ወጐየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በሀገረ ፤ አክሚም ። ወሶበ ፡ አጥፍኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሉልያኖስ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡፤ ኀሠሥዎ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ” ። ወሶበ ፡ ኢረከብዎ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አብ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፣ 'ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፤ እስመ” ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አክሚም ። ወሖሩ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወአምጽእዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበሩ” ፡ በዳኅና ፡ ወሰላም ፡ እስከ ፡ አዕረፈ ።
The emperor sent an officer who apprehended this father and imprisoned him with the patriarch of the city of Romë (= Rome) and the patriarch of the city of Ansokiyä (= Antioch). God sent his angel who delivered them from prison. After the emperor had died, God appointed his son in his place; he was of the right faith. He brought this father back to his seat, and he remained for twenty-two years in tranquility and peace. When Qestos (= Constantius II) died, Lulyänos (= Julian) the apostate reigned after him and sought this saint abbä ‘Atnäsyos. He fled far from him into Upper Egypt and remained in the city of ‘Akmim (= Akhmim). When God caused Lulyänos the apostate to perish, the people sought this father abbä ‘Atnäsyos. As they could not find him, they went to the eminent father abbä ‘Entonyos (= Anthony). Abbä ‘Entonyos said to them: "He is staying in the city of ‘Akmim." They went to him and brought him to his seat in great joy. He remained on his seat in calm and peace until he died.
ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ዓወዒዓመተ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ዘበጽሖ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ እምነ ፡ ጸማ ፡ ወምንዳቤ ፡ ወሐዘን ፡ ወስደት ፡ ተሠምየ ፡ ሐዋርያዌ ። ወበጊዜ ፡ ፅረፍቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ረከብኩ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወሞገሠ ፡ በኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር” ፡ እሰግድ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወእስእሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጥፍእ” ፡ 'ምኩራበ ፡ ሰራቤል ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ” ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአንሐሎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ምኩራብ” ፡ በጸሎቱ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡፣ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ሐዋርያዊ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
All the days of his office were forty-seven years. Because of the labors, trials, affliction, and persecution that befell this father, he was called Apostolic. At the moment of his death, he said: "If I find grace and favor with God, I will prostrate myself before him and ask him to destroy the temple of Saräbël (= Serapis)." After the death of this father abbä ‘Atnäsyos, the emperor dispatched and had this temple overturned through his prayer. May God have mercy on us through the prayer of this father abbä ‘Atnäsyos the Apostolic, the patriarch, and may his blessing be with us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 8TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ቿለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ስንሑት ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ሐና ። ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ አባግዓ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአርአዮ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፡ አንተ ፡ ትነብር ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፥፡ ስፉህ! ፡ ወአክሊላት ፡ ድልወት ፡ ለዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡፤ ተንሥእ ፡ ወሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አትሬብ"፡ ፡ ወተጋደል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወተኣምፕሙ ፡ ለአበዊሁ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አትሬብ' ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ ለመኩንን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ። ወሶበ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ፡ ተአምነ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ 'አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ገሃደ ፤ አአምን" ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became a martyr the saint abbä Yohannes (= John), who was from the city of Senhut (= Senhout). The name of the father of this saint was Magäres (= Macarius) and the name of his mother, Hannä (= Anne). While he was tending his father's sheep, the angel of God appeared to him, showed him a crown of light, and said to him: "Why are you sitting? Behold, the struggle has spread, and crowns are prepared for whoever struggles for the name of Christ. Now arise, go to the city of ‘Atrëb (= Athribis), and struggle for the name of Christ." Then he gave him the greeting and left him. Immediately this saint abbä Yohannes arose and said goodbye to his parents. He went to the city of ‘Atrëb and found the governor at the baths. When the governor came out of the baths, he confessed our Lord Jesus Christ before him, saying: "I am a Christian openly; I believe in my Lord and my God Jesus Christ, Son of the living God." He handed him over to one of his soldiers and ordered

him to flatter him in the hope that he would turn from his sentiment and obey him. Then the governor went to his business. The soldier took the saint and led him to his house. The saint performed great miracles before him, and the soldier believed in our Lord Jesus Christ. When the governor returned from his business, the soldier confessed our Lord Jesus Christ and received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. He ordered the saint Yohannes to be tortured, and they subjected him to great torture with every kind of torture. God strengthened him, made him constant, and sent his angel who healed his wounds and raised him up unharmed, without damage. After that, he sent him to the city of ‘Andënäw (= Antinoe), and there they subjected him to great torture. When the governor was weary of torturing him, he ordered his head to be cut off. They cut off his holy head with the sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Yulyos (= Julius), who was from the city of ‘Agfâhes (= Agfahs), took him, buried him, and sent him to Sanhut, his city. All the people went out, welcomed him in great joy with songs, incense, and canticles, and placed him in the church. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us, amen.
በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተከለሉ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ማኅበራነ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ፪፻፳ወ8 ሉሊስ ፡ ወማትያስ” ፡ ንቡረ ፡ እድ ፡ ዘሲሐት” ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕርገቱ ፣፤ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ እምኔነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ መካን ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ጌር ፡ ወመንፈሱ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ኪነ ፡ ጥበቡ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ በሕማማቲሁ ፡ ወበሞቱ ፡ ወትንሣኤሁ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፤ ጓመዓልት ፡ እምትንሣኤሁ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ክነፊሆሙ ፡ ለኪሩቤል” ፡ ወሰረረ ። ወሰረረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ክነፊሆሙ ፡ ለነፋሳት ። ወበዝንቱ ፡ ፅርገት ፤ ኮኖሙ” ፡ ለእለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዕርገተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ማኅደረ ፤ ልዑላት” ። እስመ ፡ ሀበ ፡፤ ዘሀሎ ፡፣ ርእስ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ መልያልያት ፡ ወበከመ ፡፣ አዳም ፡ ቀዳማዊ” ፡ በደረ" ፡ ኅበ ፡ 'ምድረ ፡ ርስት” ፡ ወኅዲረ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ አዳም ፡ ዳግማዊ ፡ በደረ” ፡ ኅበ ፡ ርስተ ፡ መንግሥት"! ፡ ሰማያዊት” ፡ ወነቢር ፡ በየማነ ፡ ዕበይ ፤ ውስተ ፣ መልዕልተ ፡፤ ሰማያት ። ወኢረሰየ ፡፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡፣ ፅዕርገቶ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትተልዋ ፡
[On this day also were crowned the 224 martyrs, companions of Yohannes (= John), Lulis, and Mätyäs (= Matthew) the priest of Sihat (= Scetis). May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear ‘Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.] On this day also took place the ascension of our Lord Jesus Christ with the body that he had received from us, up to the heavens, the place where he is with his excellent Father and his Holy Spirit. This took place thus: after having accomplished the work of his wisdom on earth with his passion, his death, and his resurrection, and when forty days were completed after his resurrection, he ascended to the heavens, seated on the wings of the cherubim, and he flew away. He flew away on the wings of the winds. Through this ascension, there came to those who believed in him the ascension into the heavens, the dwelling of the exalted. Indeed, where the head is, it is fitting that the members should be likewise. Just as the first ‘Adäm (= Adam) went as a forerunner toward the Promised Land and the sojourn in hell, so the second ‘Adäm went as a forerunner toward the inheritance of the kingdom of heaven and the sojourn at the right hand of the Greatness on high in the heavens. Our Lord—glory to him!—did not make his ascension something that followed his resurrection so that the impious would not think or say that his ascension was an illusion. But our Lord, in the operation of his wisdom, remained forty days after his resurrection to strengthen


[p. 226] {#po.47.p226}
ለትንሣኤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየሐልዩ ፡ ዓላዊያን ፡ ወኢይበሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዕርገቱ ፡ ምትሀት” ፡ ይእቲ ። አላ ፡ እግዚእነ” ፡ በኪነ ፡ ጥበቡ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ጓመዓልተ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ትንሣኤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያጸንዕ ፡ ድካመ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ ወያጠይቅ ፡ ትንሣኤሁ ፣ ለነፍሳቲሆሙ ። ወተፈጸመ ፡ በፅርገቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ፆበላዕለ ፡ ደመና ፡ ሰማይ ፡ እስከ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሉየ ፡ መዋዕል ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ስልጣነ ፡ ወመንግሥተ ፡ ወክብረ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ያምልክዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ወሕዝብ ፡ ወልሳናተ ፡ በሐውርት ፡ ወስልጣኑ ፡ ስልጣን ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ወመንግሥቱ ፡ ዘኢየሐልቅ ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡
the weakness of the heart of his disciples and to make his resurrection certain to their souls. Through the ascension of our Lord was fulfilled the prophecy of Dän’ël (= Daniel) the prophet, who says: "I saw as the Son of man coming on the clouds of heaven until he was near the Ancient of days. He gave him dominion, kingship, honor, and caused all nations, peoples, and languages of the lands to worship him. His dominion will be an eternal dominion, and his kingship will not pass away." To him belongs the glory with his excellent Father and his life-giving Holy Spirit for ever and ever, amen.
ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ቴር ፡ ወመንፈሱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማሕየዊ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ”" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ አባ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ዘገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ ኮነ ፡ ንጹሐ ፣ ወፍጹመ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሰምዓ" ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ አንስጣስያ ፡ ሥይምት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትለብስ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ መስፍን፤ ፡ ወመንኩሰት ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ወነበረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐት ፡ በቅሩብ” ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ፳ወቿ ዓመተ ፡ ወኢያእመረ ፡ ባቲ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ። ወርእዮ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለ8ብእሲ” ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አውለጊስ” ፡ ዘይወቅር ፡ አእባነ” ፡ በቂራጠ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ዕለተ" ፡ ወይሴሰይ"' ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ በበንስቲት ፣ ወይሴስዮሙ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ በዘ ፡ ተረፈ ፡ ወዘ ፡ ተረፈ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ይወግሮ ፡ ለከለባት ። ወኢኮነ ፡ ይዘግብ ፤ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሳኒታ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፤ አሠነየ” ፡ ገድሎ ፡፤ ወአደሞ ፡ ግብሩ ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ እምኅበ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ ለአውለጊስ” ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትወሰክ”፤ ፡ ቦቱ” ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወምሕረት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተሀበዮ ፡ ለአውለጊስ” ። ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይወቅር ፡ አአባነ ፡ ረከበ ፤ 'መዝገበ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወነሥኦ” ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀ-ስጥንጥንያ ። ወኮነ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ለንጉሥ”' ፡ ወኀደገ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀስጥንጥንያ ፡ ወርእዮ ፡ ለአውለጊስ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡፤ ሊቀ” ፡ ሐራ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹዓን ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፈረስ ፡ ወየዐውድዎ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰራዊት ፡ በትዕቢት ፡ አቢይ ፡ ወኀደገ ፡ ገቢረ” ፡ ሠናየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ይነብር ፡እንዘ ፡ ይቤንኖሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስቅልዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ወኅሠሠፆ፤ ፡ እምኔሁ” ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ለአውለጊስ” ። ወዘከመ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ሰአለቶ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡፤ በእንቲአሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሀ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ ገብአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ አውለጊስ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚጦ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንዴቱ ፡ 'በከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ” ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡
On this day also died the holy father abbä Dän’ël (= Daniel), superior of the desert of ‘Asqêtes (= Scetis). This saint was pure and perfect. When his fame had spread, the holy ‘Anestäsyä (= Anastasia) the patrician came to him dressed in the clothes of a dignitary; she became a monk with him, remained in a cell near him for twenty-eight years, and no one knew that she was a woman. This saint saw a man by the name of ‘Awlagis (= Eulogius) who cut stones for a small gold coin each day; he fed himself on a small part of it, fed the poor and the needy with almost all that remained, and what was left over, he threw to the dogs. He reserved nothing for the next day. When this saint saw him, he found his spiritual combat beautiful, and his action pleased him. He asked God to give ‘Awlagis the goods of this world so that his charity and his mercy might be increased. Then he became a guarantor for ‘Awlagis. While the latter was cutting stones, he found a treasure of gold, took it, and went to the city of Questentenyä (= Constantinople). He became a great army commander there for the emperor and abandoned the beautiful actions he had performed before. When the saint abbä Dän’ël heard this about him, he went to the city of Questentenyä; he saw how ‘Awlagis had become a great army commander—mounted on a horse, many soldiers surrounding him, with great pride—and had abandoned charity. Then this saint saw a vision: our Lord Jesus Christ was seated judging men; he ordered the saint abbä Dän’ël to be suspended and demanded from him the soul of ‘Awlagis. And our Lady Mary begged our Lord Christ on his behalf. When this saint awoke, he returned to his monastery and begged God concerning ‘Awlagis so that he would bring him back to his poverty as he was before. The angel of God appeared to him and enjoined him not to defy God regarding the judgment of the latter over his creation. After that, the emperor of Questentenyä died, another emperor reigned in his place, rose up against ‘Awlagis, took all his goods, and wanted to kill him.


[p. 228] {#po.47.p228}
እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወገሠጾ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትሐበል ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኩነኔሁ ፡ ላዕለ ፤ ፍጥረቱ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ቀ“ስጥንጥንያ” ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ሀየንቴሁ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አውለጊስዎ ፣፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትሎ ። ወጐየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅን ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፤ ወኮነ” ፡ ይወቅር ፡ አአባነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወተጋብአ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡
('Awlagis) fled far from him to save his life, arrived in his own country, and began to carve stones as before. The holy abba Dän'ël met with him and told him how he had been suspended because of him in a vision and how his (= 'Awlagis's) soul had been demanded of him.
ዳንኤል ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ሰቀልዎ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ በራእይ ፡ ወዘከመ ፣ የኀሥሥ" ፡ ነፍሶ”፤ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ ፤ ተአምራተ” ፡ ብዙኃተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ዕልዋን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅእዎ ፡ እምሃይማኖት” ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ወአበየ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ውእተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ዘውስቴቱ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ፅልዋን ፤ ወበተኮ ። ወኩነንዎ” ፡፣ ሐራሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፎ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ጸአቱ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ። ወአስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፣፤ ወአጽንዖሙ” ፡ ወናዘዞሙ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
The spirit of prophecy was upon this saint, and God caused many miracles to appear through his hands. When the impious wanted to force him out of the right faith, he refused, seized the writing in which the faith of the impious (was set forth), and tore it up. The emperor's soldiers subjected him to many tortures, and after that, when God wanted to give him rest, He sent His angel to him, and (the angel) informed him of the time of his departure from this world. (The saint) gathered the monks, gave them recommendations, strengthened them, consoled them, and died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መክሲ ፡ መስተጋድል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also the holy Maksi (= Maximus?) the (spiritual) combatant became a martyr. May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 9TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ዘለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ እሌኒ ፡ ንግሥት ። ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሀ፤ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፤ ወሐጸንዋ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ወመሀርዋ ፡ ጽሕፈተ ፡ ወትምሕርተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወኮነ ፡ አርአያሃ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወውስክት ፡ 'በሠናይ ፡ ነፍሳ ፡ ወሥጋሃ" ። ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ቆንስጣ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ በርንጥያ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሀ" ፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሃ ፡ ወትሩፋቲሃ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ አርአያሃ ። ወኀሠሣ ፡ ወአውሰባ ፡ 'ወወለደት ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ 'ቀዳሜ ፡ ነገሥተ' ፤ ክርስቲያን ። ወሐጸነቶ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተሐጽኖ ፡ ወመሀረቶ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ወአእምሮ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ርእየት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በንዋም ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይቤላ ፡ ሖሪ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወክሥቲ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ወመካናተ ፡ ቅዱሳተ” ። ወአይድዓቶ ፡ ለወልዳ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእየት ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ሰራዊተ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወኃተተት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ማሕየዊ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ረከበቶ ፡ ወረከበት ፡-ምስሌሁ ፡ ፪መሳቅላተ ፡ እለ ፡ ተሰቅሉ ፡ዲቤሁ ፡ ፈያታዊያን ። ወፈቀደት ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the pure (and) holy 'Elëni (= Helena) the empress. This saint was from the people of the city of Roha (= Edessa). Her parents were Christians; they raised her in goodness and taught her the art of writing and the teaching of the Church. Her appearance was beautiful, and her soul and body grew in beauty. In those days, Qonstä (= Constantius), king of Barnetyä (= Britain), arrived at the city of Roha. He learned the history and virtues of this saint and that her appearance was beautiful. He asked for her hand in marriage and married her. She bore him Quastantinos (= Constantine) the emperor, the first of the Christian emperors. She raised him with a fine education and taught him philosophy and science. When he reigned, she saw in (her) sleep as if someone said to her: "Go to Jerusalem and discover the Cross and the holy Places." She made known to her son what she had seen, and he sent soldiers with her to Jerusalem. She searched for the wood of the life-giving Cross until she had found it, and she found with it the two crosses upon which the thieves had been crucified. She wanted to know which was the cross of Christ. The holy abba Magäres (Macarius), bishop of the city of Jerusalem, taught her that it was the cross at the head of which (was) a piece of wood on

ከመ ፡ ታእምር ፡ አይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መስቀሉ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወአይድዓ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ መስቀል ፡ ዘመልዕልተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ወጽሑፍ ፡ ዲቤሃ" ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ አይሁድ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈቀደት ፡ ከመ ፡ ትርአይ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ትዕምርተ ፡ ወይትፌሣሕ ፤ ልባ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ በፈቃደ" ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ አምጽኡ" ፡ ፳ምውተ ፡ ወአንበረት”" ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፪መስቀላተ ፡ ወኢተንሥአ” ። ወአንበረት” ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ መስቀሎ ፡ ለመድኃኒነ ፡፤ ወተንሥአ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወተወሰከ ፡ ሃይማኖታ ፡ ወፍሥሐሃ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወእምዝ" ፡ ሐለየት፤' ፡ ለሕንጸተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት” ፡እለ ፡ ጽሑፋት ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወኒ ለመስከረም ። ወእምዝ ፤ መጠወት ፡ ለአባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕንጽ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡፤ ወኩሎ ፡ መካናተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥአት ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ወቅንዋተ ፡ ወተመይጠት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ወመጠወቶ ፡ ሎቱ ። 'ወተፈሥሐ ፡፣ ንጉሥ" ፡ ዓቢየ ፤ ፍሥሐ ፤ ወተኣምፕ ፡ ለመስቀል ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወአክበሮ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ስርግው ፡ በዕንቁ ፡፤ ባሕርይ ። ወነሥአ ፡ ቅንዋተ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ጌራ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልጓመ ፡ ፈረሱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትፈጸም ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘጽሑፍ ፡ እስመ ፡ ልጓመ ፡ ፈረሱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ መድኀኒት ። ወተጋደለት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወአሥመረቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወሠርዐት ፤
(which was) written: "This is Jesus, King of the Jews." Then she wanted to see a miracle from it: her heart would rejoice. At that moment, by the will of our Lord Jesus Christ, a dead man was brought. She placed the two crosses (of the thieves) upon him, and he did not rise. She placed the cross of our Savior upon him, and he rose immediately. Her faith and her joy increased thereby. Then she occupied herself with the construction of the churches which (are mentioned) in writing on the 17th of Maskaram. Then she handed over to abba Magäres much money to build the churches and all the (holy) Places. Then she took the glorious Cross and the nails, returned to Quastantinos her son, and handed them over to him. The emperor rejoiced with great joy; he kissed the glorious Cross, honored it greatly, and had a reliquary made for it of gold adorned with precious stones. He took the nails and put some in his helmet and some in the bit of his horse so that the word which (is) written might be fulfilled: in the bit of the king's horse is salvation. This saint fought every good (spiritual) fight and pleased God. She established many foundations for churches and monasteries and for the food and clothing of the poor and the needy. All the days of her life were eighty years, and she died in peace. May her prayer be with us, amen.
ጐልታተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ለአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ወለአድባራት ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ፡ ወአልባሲሆሙ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወታ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ወአዕረፈት ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለበይደር” ፡ ወስልዋኖስ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of Baydar and Selwänos (= Silvanus). May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 10TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ፲ለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ክቡራን ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኮደቂቅ ፡ አናንያ ፡ ወአዛርያ ፤ ወሚሳኤል ። እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ኢዮአቄም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፤ ወዳንኤል ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እኅቶሙ ። ወፄወውዎምጮ፤ ፡ ለኢዮአቄም ፡ ወለውሉዱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ። ወኀረየ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እምፄዋዌ ፡ ውሉድ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡፣ እስራኤል ፤ ወራዙተ ፡ ሠናያነ ፡ አርአያሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕጽኖሙ ፡ በመብልዕ ፣ ወመስቴ ፡ ወይሬስዮሙ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰራዊቱ ። ወኮኑ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ኀረዮሙ" ፡ እሉ ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ወዳንኤል ፡ ወልደ ፡ እኅቶሙ ። ወኢፈቀዱ ፡ እሉ ፡ ጸድ ቃን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብልዑ ፡ እምዝቡሐት ፡ ባዕዳን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዝቡሐተ ፡ ደቂቀ ፣
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, (there is) the commemoration of the illustrious (and) holy righteous ones, the three children 'Anänyä (= Hananiah), 'Azäryä (= Azariah), and Misä'êl (= Mishael). These saints were sons of 'lyo'aqêm (= Jehoiakim), king of Yehudä (= Judah), and Dän'el (= Daniel) was the son of their sister. 'lyo'aqêm and his sons were taken into captivity to the city of Bäbilon (= Babylon). The king chose from among the captives young men, children of Israel, adolescents of beautiful appearance, to raise them with food and drink and to make them soldiers with his guards. Among those he chose were these righteous ones and Dän'êl, the son of their sister. These righteous ones did not want to eat any meat other than the meat of the children of Israel. They said to the chief of the soldiers to let them not eat meat-based food but to give them green vegetables. The chief of the soldiers said to them: "I fear that the appearance of your face might be changed and the king might execute me." They answered him and

said to him: "Put us to the test: if our face is not beautiful, do what you want." Then they fed on vegetables, and their faces shone and were very beautiful by the grace of God. The king loved them much and appointed them governors over all the land of Bäbilon. When the king made a statue of gold, he ordered all the people to prostrate themselves before it. As the three holy young men did not prostrate themselves before it, those who were jealous of them slandered them to the king. Nabukednäsor (= Nebuchadnezzar) the king had them brought and questioned them about this. They confessed (God) before him and said to him: "We do not prostrate ourselves before a statue of gold." The king became angry, gave an order, and they were thrown into a furnace of burning fire. God sent His angel, and (the angel) extinguished the flames of the fire and made the furnace of fire like fresh dew at the time of the morning. After the flames of fire had risen to forty-nine cubits, they burned those who were outside the furnace of fire, and the fire did not touch the three holy young men. When the king saw this, he believed in the God of heaven, increased their (= the young men's) honors and their elevation, and raised their position.

When it was the 10th of the month of Genbot, while they were praying in their cell, when they prostrated themselves, they surrendered their souls into the hand of God. At that moment, there was a great earthquake in the city of Bäbilon. King Näbukednäsor was afraid and asked Dän'êl the prophet why this great earthquake had taken place. Dän'ël informed the king and said to him: "The three holy young men are dead." The king went to them and felt great affliction for them. He ordered three sarcophagi of elephant bone to be made for them, to bury them in garments of silk (and) brocade, and to place them therein (= in the sarcophagi). It was done for them as (the king had ordered). He also ordered a sarcophagus of gold to be made for himself so that when he died, his body would be placed therein and placed between the bodies of the saints. It was so. When it was the time of the father abba Tëwoflos (= Theophilus), the patriarch of the city of Alexandria, he built a church for them in their name and wanted to bring their bodies to this church. He sent abba Yohannes (= John) the Short to them. When (the latter) arrived at the city of Bäbilon, he saw its rivers; absolutely no man was (to be found) there, and the statue of gold had remained there. The angel of God brought him to the bodies of the saints; the body of the king (was) between them. (Yohannes) prostrated himself upon the bodies, wept, and said: "O my holy fathers, our father abba Tëwoflos the patriarch has built a church in your name and wants to bring your bodies there." A voice came out of their bodies saying: "May God give you the reward

ሐነጸ ፤ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፤ በስምክሙ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ያብጽሕ ፤ ሥጋክሙ ፡ ኅቤሃ” ። ወወፅአ ፡ ቃል ፡እምሥጋሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ይብል ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡የሀብከ ፡ እሜተ ፡ ጸማከ ፡ በሎ ፡ ለአበ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ አባ ፣ ቴዎፍሎስ ፡፣ እስመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አዘዘነ ፡ ከመ ፡ "ኢይትፈለጥ ፤ ሥጋነ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ መካን” ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ደይን ። አላ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ኢንሬሲ ፡ጸጻማሁ ፡ይትሐጐል ፡ ንግሮ ፡ ያአዝዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስቅሉ ፡ ማሕወ ፡ በሌሊተ” ፡ ቅዳሴሃ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወይረስዩ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ቅብአ ፣ ወኢያኅትውዎሙ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወንሕነ ፡ ንበጽሕ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወንከሥት ፡ ኅይለነ ፡ ባቲ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ” ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አዘዝዎጆ ፡ ቅዱሳን” ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወገብሩ” ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዙ ፡ ፻ደቂቅ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወበሌሊት” ፡ ፲ለወርኃ ፡ ግንቦት ፣ አስተርአዩ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፤ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአኅተዉ ፡ ዙሎ ፣ በእሳት ። ወኮነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወእለ ፡ ይደልዎሙ ፡ ይኔጽርዎ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ኮደቂቅ ፡እንዘ ፡ የዐውዱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወብዙኃን ፡ 'እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ድውያነ ፡ በዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ተፈወሱ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ደቂቅ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡፣ ወትንብልናሆሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡
for your labor: tell the father of the faith, abba Tëwoflos, that God has prescribed for us that our bodies would not leave this place until the day of Judgment. But we will not let his labor be lost: tell him to order glass (lamps) to be hung on the night of the consecration of the church, to put oil in them, and not to light them with fire. And we, we will come there and reveal our power there." When abba Yohannes had returned to the patriarch, he told him everything the saints had prescribed for him. The patriarch gave orders, and it was done as the three holy young men (had prescribed). On the tenth night of the month of Genbot, the saints appeared in the church and lit all (the lamps) with fire. The patriarch and those who were worthy (of it) saw the three holy young men circulating during the consecration of the church. Many of those who were sick with different diseases were then healed thanks to the three holy young men. May God have mercy on us through their prayer and through their intercession, and may their blessing be with us forever, amen.
ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለቴክላ” ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወፎላ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of Teklä (= Thecla) the martyr and (of) Folä. May their prayer be with their dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ፀራቢ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ መርቅያስ ፡ ዘትሰመይ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ። ወላዲቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወሀበቶ ፡ ለመምህር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመሀር ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወከሠተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ልቡ ፤ ወተምህረ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወወጠነ ፡ ጾመ ፡ ወጸሎተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ተምህረ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ጽርበት ፡ ዘምክያደ ፡ አዕባን ፡ ወምህዋረ ፡ ማይ ። ወበዝንቱ ፡ አጥረየ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይመጸውት ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡እንዘ ፡ይጹሊ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተዘከረ ፡ ኅልፈተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘውስቴታ ፡ፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡'በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ” ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፣ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ፅናዕ ፡ ወኢትፍራህ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትመውኦሙ ፡ ለፀርከ ፡ ወትነሥእ ፡ አክሊለ ፤ ስምዕ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ሥዕሉ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ነዋኃ ፡ ወአማኅፀነ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምዥናን ፣ ወአምነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አሰርዎ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ድኅሪተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፅፀው ፡ ክቡዳን ፡ ወወገርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ወጽምእ ። ወጊዜ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ለዘይጹውእ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ወይገብር ፡ ተዝካሮ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ክሳዶ ። ወሶበ ፡ መተርዎ ፡ ሰረረት ፡ ርእሱ ፤ መልዕልተ ፡ ጉቡኣን ፡ እንዘ ፤
[On this day also died 'Abrehäm (= Abraham) the stone-cutter. This saint was from the people of the city of Margeyäs, which is called the monastery of the martyr Téwodros (= Theodore). The mother of this little child gave him to a teacher so that he might learn the faith of our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! God opened the eyes of his heart, and he learned all the law of the Church and began fasting and prayer. When he was nine years old, he learned the trade of cutting stone thresholds and water conduits. Thanks to this, he earned much money, and he gave alms to the poor and the needy. One day, while he was praying at night, when he remembered the end of the world and (of) everything that (is) in it, he wanted to become a martyr for our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! Then the angel of God appeared to him, gave him the greeting, and said to him: "Be strong and do not be afraid: you will conquer your enemies and you will receive the crown of martyrdom." Then he went to the church of the saint Téwodros, received Christian baptism, stood before his (= Theodore's) image, prayed for a long time, and entrusted himself to him. He went to the seat of the governor and confessed that he was a Christian. Immediately they bound his hands behind his back with heavy pieces of wood and threw him into the (full) sun with hunger and thirst (to endure). At the time of the middle of the night, our Lord appeared to him and gave him a covenant for whoever would invoke his name and perform his commemoration. Then the governor


[p. 236] {#po.47.p236}
ትጸርኅ ፡ ወትብል ፡ ፻ጊዜ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ አነ ፡ ወሖረት ፡፤ መጠነ ፡ ዛእመት ፡ ወወድቀት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሠጠቁ ፡ ከርሦ ፡ ወመልእዎ ፡ ቅጥራነ ፡ ወፒሳ ፡ ወዘይተ ፡ ውዑየ ፡ ወወገርም" ፡ ውስተ ፡ እቶነ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወኢለከፎ ፡፣ ወኢምንትኒ ። ወእምዝ ፡ መተርዎ ፡ በበንስቲት ፡ ወወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከፈር ፡ ወወረውዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ። ወበፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስና ፡ እንዘ ፡ ልጹቅ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ርእሱ ። ወነሥእዎ ፣ መሃይምናን ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ክብር ። ጸሎቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
ordered his neck to be cut. When it had been cut, his head flew above (those who were) gathered, crying out and saying three times: "I am a Christian." And it advanced for fifty cubits and fell into the river. Then they opened his belly, filled it with bitumen, pitch, and burning oil, and threw him into a furnace of fire. Nothing touched him. Then they cut him into small (pieces), put him in a basket, and threw him into the river. By the will of God, his body, joined to his head, appeared on the shore of the city of 'Esnä (= Esna). Believers took it and buried it with great honor. May his prayer be with his dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

[On this day (also), there is the commemoration of the consecration of the church of abba Misä'ël (= Michael?). The parents of this saint were Christians and rich in goods. As they had no child, they implored God and begot this saint. When he had grown up, they taught him the books of the Church. When his parents died, the bishop of the city kept his goods for him. When he was twelve years old, he remembered the end of the world. He went to the monastery of abba Yeshaq (= Isaac) and asked him for the monastic state. After having tested him and having seen his holiness, (Yeshaq) made him a monk. The grace of the Holy Spirit descended upon him, and he tired himself with fasting and prayer to the point that the brightness of his face was altered and his body became like dry wood. One night, abba Yeshaq went to his dwelling. When he saw his appearance, which had become like (that of) a dead man, he wept and said to him: "How have you destroyed yourself thus?" The saint said to him: "Do not weep, my father, for God has not abandoned me and my strength has not weakened because of standing. But I will tell you what I saw in my dream: envoys of the emperor will come to you from Alexandria seeking me. Do not prevent them so that they do not do you harm. The second year a great famine will occur and it will devastate the land; gather food for your (spiritual) children." A little (time) later, men came under the appearance of soldiers of the emperor. They seized abba Yeshaq, asking him for Misä'ël. When they recognized the latter, they took him with them, and the monks remained in affliction. When the famine occurred, the governor of the city learned that there was wheat in the monastery, and he wanted to attack them (= the monks). Abba Yeshaq went out to him to beg him. Immediately many soldiers came out of the desert with swords and took the governor away. While abba Yeshaq was wondering about these men, abba Misä'ël came to him and showed himself to him. He told him that these men were soldiers of heaven and (that) the city to which they had led him was the heavenly Jerusalem. Then he ordered him to build a church for him with his goods that were with the bishop; (Yeshaq) built it for him in his cell, and it was consecrated on the


[p. 238] {#po.47.p238}
ይመውት” ፡ በዳግም ፡ ዓመት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቃሉ ። በረከቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
same (day) as this day. Abba Misä'ël appeared to abba Yeshaq and told him that he would die the following year, and it was according to his word. May his blessing be with his dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አባ ፡ ኖብ ፡ 'ሰማዕታት ፡ ወጻድቃን” ፡ ፻፻ወፃዛ ወሰመንቱ ፡፤ ነፍስ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፤ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፤ ወፍቅርቶሙሂ ፡ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day (also) became martyrs with abba Nob martyrs and righteous ones: three hundred and fifty-eight souls. May their blessing be with their dear 'Atnätëwos and their dear Kotolikäwit for ever and ever, amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ 'አዕረፈ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ በትረ ፡ ያርክ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also died Yohannes (= John) the patriarch. May his holy blessing be with his dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 11TH OF GENBOT

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, there is the holy Tä'okelyä (= Theoclea) the martyr, the wife of the saint Yostos (= Justus); indeed, after the governor of the city of Alexandria had separated them from each other as it is written on the 10th of Yakkätit, the holy Tä'okelyä was led to the city of Dä (= Tanis). When they read the letter of the governor of the city of Alexandria before the governor of Dä, (the latter) was astonished and said: "Why did (Yostos) abandon his royalty and (why) did they (= the spouses) choose death instead of royalty?" The governor flattered her much and made her great promises. The saint answered him and said to him: "I have abandoned my royalty and have not turned back to it; I have accepted being separated from my husband since my youth and I have consoled myself (for being separated) from my children for the love of my Lord Jesus Christ; how much will you give me, you?" He ordered her to be slapped, and she was inflicted with a great scourging to the point that her skin was torn. After that, they threw her into prison. The angel of God appeared to her and healed her wounds. When they saw her, many prisoners and other (people) were astonished, believed in our Lord Jesus Christ, and became martyrs. When the moment of the death of the saint Tä'okelyä approached, the angel of God appeared to her, consoled her, and made her many promises. At that moment, the governor ordered her head to be cut off; they cut off the head of the saint with the sword, and she received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Believers came, gave money to the soldiers, took the body of the saint, buried it in a beautiful shroud, and placed it in a sarcophagus until the end of the days of the persecution. May her prayer and her blessing be with us, amen.
የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ በፍኖትዮስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ መንኩሰ ፡ እምንአሱ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡
On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of the holy abba Bafnotyos (= Paphnuce) the bishop. This father became a monk from his childhood in the desert of
