ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ጽልመተ ፡ ክሕደት ። ወእምዝ” ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ዩልያስ" ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወኦዱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወሰበኩ ፡ ወአጥመቁ ፡፣ ብዙኃነ ፡ አሕዛበ ፤ ዘኢይትጐለቀ ። ወገብሩ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ወሰደዱ ፡ ሰይጣናተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ እምላዕለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወፈወስዎሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ድውያን ፡ ዘዕፁብ ፣፤ ደዌሆሙ ፡ ወአንሐሉ ፡ አብያተ ፤ ጣዖታት ፡ ወሐነጽዎሙ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ዘለእግዚአነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ ስብከቶሙ ፡ ወፈቀደ"! ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍልሶሙ ፡-እምሐዘን ፡ ወጻማ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወያብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘፍሥሐ ፡፤ ወሐሜት ። ወእምዝ ፤ ሐመ ፡ አንድሮኒቆስ"፤ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወሶበ ፡ ገነዞ ፡ ዩልያስ" ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ካዕበ ፣ በሳኒታሁ ።
in the darkness of infidelity. Then he took with him Yulyäs (= Julius) the apostle; they went around many cities, preached, and baptized many people, countless. They performed miracles and wonders there, cast out many demons from people, healed many sick people whose illnesses (were) grave, overturned temples of idols, and built for them (= the people) churches which (belong) to our Lord Jesus Christ. When they had finished their preaching, God wanted to make them leave the sadness and the torment of this world and make them enter into the world of joy and gladness. Then 'Anderonigos the apostle fell ill and died on the same (day) as this day. When Yulyäs the apostle had placed him in a tomb, he himself died also, the next day. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us, amen.
እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። 1ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፡ ሠራቃዊ ። በረከቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of Yä'qob (= James) the Oriental. May his blessing be with his dear 'Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 23RD OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ፳፻ለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዩልያስ' ፡ ሐዋርያ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ግብሪል" ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እምነገደ" ፡ ይሁዳ ። ኀረዮ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ እምጉጐልቆሙ ፡ ለይወ፪አርድእት ፡ ወተወክፈ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ። ወሰበከ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወበጽሖ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወስደት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አንበሩ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆኦሰ ። ወፈነውዎ ፡ ይስብክ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ብዙኅ ። ወእምድህረዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አንድሮኒቆስ" ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አንድሮኒቆስ' ፡ ገነዞ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዩል ያስ ፡ ወቀበሮ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይፍልጦ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወበሳኒታሁ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወናሁ ፡ ዘከሮሙ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ በመልእክቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በተፍጻሜታ ፡ ተአምኅዎሙ ፤
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy Yulyäs (= Julius) the apostle. This saint was by birth of Bêta Gebril, from among the children of Israel, of the tribe of Yehudä (= Judah). God chose him among the seventy-two disciples and he received the grace of the Holy Spirit, the Paraclete. He preached with the Apostles. Great tribulations and persecutions happened to him with them. After that, the Apostles laid their hands on him and consecrated him bishop. They sent him to preach the kingdom of God and he preached in many cities. After that, he went with 'Anderonigos (= Andronicus) the apostle and preached with him. After the saint 'Anderonigos had died, this saint Yulyäs shrouded him and buried him. Then he prayed to God that he might not separate him from him. The next day, this saint died. Behold, the Apostle Päwlos (= Paul) mentioned these apostles in his Epistle to the Romans when he says at the end of it: "Greet 'Anderonigos and Yolyäs." May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
ለአንድሮኒቆስ" ፡ ወዮልያስ'" ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮልያኖስ ፣ ወእሙ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አንስያ ፡ ሰማዕት ፣፡ ወአፍራዲጦስ ፡ ፳እምቋድወ፪አርድዕት ፡ ወዮልያ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወታኦድሪጦስ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ወታድሮስ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡
On this day also, the saint Yolyänos (= Julian) and his mother became martyrs in the city of Alexandria. May their prayer be with us, amen. [On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of abbä 'Anseyä the martyr, (of) 'Afräditos (= Aphrodite), one of the seventy-two disciples, (of) Yolyä (=


[p. 316] {#po.47.p316}
ወቅዱስ ፡ ዮልያኖስ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለሰማዕታት ፡ ማኅበራነ ፡ ኤስድሮስ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ዘክልኤ ፡ ወርኅ ። ወተናገረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ወልዳ ፡ አነ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እምየ ፡ ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
Julius) the martyr, (of) Tä'oderitos (= Theodoret) the priest, (of) Täderos (= Theodore) the bishop, (of) the saint Yolyänos (= Julian) and (of) his mother, martyrs in the city of Alexandria; and the commemoration of the martyrs companions of 'Ésderos (= Isidore), (his) wife and the son of the latter (aged) two months. The son of the latter spoke saying: "I (am) a Christian with my mother." Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) (had) them killed. May their blessing be with their dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR THE 24TH OF GENBOT

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this blessed day, our Lord Jesus Christ came into the land of Egypt. He was a child whose days (were) two years, just as the holy Gospel says: The angel of God appeared to Yosëf (= Joseph) in a dream saying: "Arise, take the child and his mother, go to the land of Egypt and dwell there until I speak to you." The coming of our Lord Jesus Christ into the land of Egypt took place because of two operations of his wisdom. One (is the following): if Hërodes (= Herod) the impious found him and could not kill him, (it was not necessary that) others should think that his incarnation was an illusion. As for the second, (it was) so that the people of the land of Egypt might not be deprived of his grace (manifested) by his movements in their midst, so that he might burn the idols that were in the land of Egypt, and so that the prophecy of 'Isayeyäs (= Isaiah) the prophet might be fulfilled who says: Behold, God is mounted on a light cloud, he enters into the land of Egypt, and the idols of Egypt fall before him. Our Lord fled by the operation of his wisdom; it is not out of fear that he fled. The first city where Yosëf, our Lady the Virgin Mary, our Lord Christ, and Salomë (= Salome) arrived, (a city) whose name (is) Bastä (= Bastah), did not welcome them. They dug there a spring of water which was a healing for all the people of the cities except that city. From there, they went to the monastery of Gamnudi (= Samannoud) and crossed the river toward the west. Our Lord put his heel on a stone, and the mark of his heel on this stone exists until now. This place was called Heel of Jesus. Our Lord said to his mother Mary: "Know, Mary my mother, that in this place a church will be built in my name and in your name. Miracles and wonders will appear there until the end of the world and it will be called Dabra Metmäq." From there, they went to the river, crossed toward the west, and saw from afar the mountain of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis). Our Lord Christ blessed it and said to his mother: "My mother Mary, know that, in this (place), there will be many monks, devout and ascetic, and (that) they will serve

ምሥራቅ ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ በትር ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትመረቱጐዝ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ 'አመረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዮሴፍ" ፡ ወሀቦ" ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ በትር ። ወሶበ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ ይቤልዋ”፤ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ንንበር ፡ ዝየ ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ገዳም" ፡ ወዕውቅ' ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ዓይነ ፡ "ፀሐይ ፡ ወይእቲ” ፡ መጠርያ ። ወእግዚእነሰ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ በትሮ ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ ወሰበራ ፡፣ ወረሰያ ፡ በበሥባር ፡ ንኡሳን ፡ ወተከሎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ወከረየ ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ መለኮታዊት ፡ ዓዘቅተ ፤ ወውሕዘ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ማይ ፡ ጥዑም ፡ ወምዑዝ ፡ ጹናሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወነሥአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ በእራሓቲሁ ፡ ወአስተዮሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ስባረ ፡ ዕፀው ፡ ዘተከሎሙ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ሠረጹ ፡ ወጹነወ ፡፤ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ጹና ፡ ምዐዝ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፤ ዘይምዕዝ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ዕፍረታት ። ወልህቁ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ዕፀው ፡ ወሰመዮ ፡ በለሳም ። ወይቤላ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ኦእምየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ በለሳም ፡ ዘተከልናሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ዝየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወእምኔሁ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ቅብአ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፣ ለእለ ፡ ይጠመቁ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ።
God like the angels." From there, they arrived at the mountain of the East. There was in the hand of Yosëf a staff on which he leaned. Our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him!—made a sign toward Yosëf (and the latter) gave him the staff. When (Jesus) had taken it, they said to his mother: "Let us sit here." This place was deserted and known before (by the name of) "Eye of the sun" (= Heliopolis), it is Mataryä (= Matarieh). Our Lord took the staff of Yosëf, broke it, put it into small pieces, and planted these in this place. He dug with his divine hands a well and sweet water flowed from it whose odor (was) very sweet. Our Lord took some of this water in his palms and watered the pieces of wood that he had planted. Immediately they sprouted and there emanated from them a very sweet odor, sweeter than all perfumes. These (pieces of) wood grew and (Jesus) called them "balsam." He said to our Lady Mary: "My mother, this balsam that we have planted will remain here forever. From it will come the chrism of Christian baptism for those who will be baptized in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit.
ወእምህየ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ብህንሳ ፡ ወኀበ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘይሰመይ ፡ በይሱስ ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሁ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዓዘቅታተ ፡ ዘይፌውሱ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ደዌ ፣ ወኩሎ ፡ ሕማመ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ረሰየ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ዓዘቅት ፡ "ትእምርተ ፡ ለባሕረ” ፡ ግብጽ” ፡ ለለዓመት ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ፅጣን ፡፣ በመንፈቀ ፡ መዓልት ፡ ሶበ ፡ 'ይጹልዩ ፤ ካህናት” ፣ ወያዓርጉ” ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዓዘቅት ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ይትፌጸም” ፡ ምንባበ ፡ ወንጌል” ፡ የዓርግኞ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዓዘቅት ፡ ወይበጽሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ አፉሃ ፡ ወይትባረኩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካኑ ፡ በከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወይሰፍርዎ ፡ በእመት፣፤ እምኅበ ፡ ዘይበጽሕ”' ፡ ማይ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይነብር ፡ በመትሕተ ፡ ዓዘቅት ፡ ወትረ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፤ ፳በእመት ፡ ይከውን ፡ ጽጋብ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዓመት ፡ አው ፡ ፲ወቿበእመት ፡ አው ፡ ፲ወበእመት ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ጽጋብ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ፲ወፄ5በእመት ፡'አው ፡ ፲ወ፳በእመት ፡ ውእቱሰ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ረኀብ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረማ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወእምህየ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስሙናይን ፡ ወሰበረ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ እለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ውስቴታ ። ወነበሩ ፡ በህየ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አፍሎን ። ወሀለዉ ፡ በህየ ፡ ዕፀወ ፡ ኮሞል ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ድኑናነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወእምህየ ፡ ሐሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሞስቋም ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ሄአውራኃ ። ወረሰየ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ዓዘቅታተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ይፈውሱ” ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ደዌ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ነቢረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ፻ዓመት ፡ ወፄአውራኅ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ሄሮድስ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ በሕልም ፡ካዕበ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ወንሥኦ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡ ወለእሙ ፡ ወሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወሶበ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ እምህየ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ መሐርቃ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምስር ፡ ኃደሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓት ፡ እንተ ፡ይእቲ ፡ 'ይእዜ ፡ ውስተ፤ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡
From there, they went to the city of Behnesä (= Behnesah) to a place which is called Baysus, which, translated, means the "House of Jesus." Our Lord made wells there that healed all illnesses and all evils. He also put in a well a mark for the river of Egypt, for each year. This (takes place) thus: at the moment of the prayer of incense, in the middle of the day, when the priests pray and offer incense to God near this well, when the reading of the Gospel is finished, the water which (is) in the well rises and reaches the mouth of it. (The people) are blessed by it and, immediately, it returns to its location, as (it was) previously. One measures the water with a cubit from where it reaches to where it remains constantly at the bottom of the well. If there are twenty cubits, a great abundance occurs in the land of Egypt that year; or (if there are) eighteen cubits or seventeen cubits, likewise abundance occurs. If there are sixteen cubits or fifteen cubits, a great famine occurs in the land of Egypt. From there, they went to the city of 'Esmunäyn (= Eschmounein); our Lord broke the idols that were there. They remained there a few days with a man by the name of 'Aflon (= Apollon?). There were persea trees there, they prostrated themselves before our Lord Christ and they have (remained) inclined until this day. From there, they went to Dabra Quesquäm and remained there six months. Our Lord put wells of water there so that they might heal all illnesses. When our Lord had finished remaining in the land of Egypt the days he wanted, that is to say three years and six months, Hërodes died. The angel of God appeared again to Yosëf in a dream and said to him: "Arise, take the child and his mother and go into the land of Israel." When they returned from there, they arrived at the city of Maharegäà (= Moharraq). When they arrived at Mesr (= Old Cairo),

ዘቅዱስ ፡ ሰርጊዮስ” ፡ በምስር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ እምስር ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መጠርያ ፡ ወተሐፅቡ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡፣ ወኮነት ፡፣ ይእቲ ፡ ነቅዓ ፡ ማይ ፡ ቅድስት ፣፤ ወቡርክት ፣ እምይእቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ወዓንቅዓ” ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ነገርነ” ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወእምህየ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ ቅብዓ ፡ በልሳም ፡ ዘተከለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፤ ይትፌጸም" ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፤ ወምሥዋዕት ፡ ወንዋየ ፡ ቅድሳት ። ወእምኔሁ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ መድኃኒታተ ፡ ወፈውሰ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ወይሁብዎ ፤ አምኃ ፡ለነገሥት ፡ ወይትሜክሑ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወእምህየ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምኅፃብ ። ወተፈጸመ ፡ በተመይጦቱ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡ 'ለዖዝያ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል” ፡ እምግብጽ ፡ ጸዋዕክዎ ፡ ለወልድየ ። ወይደልወነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንግበር ፡ በዓለ ፣ መንፈሳዊተ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወንዘምር ፣ ውስቴቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ በግብጽ” ፡ ወመንክረ ፡ በሐቅለ ፡ ጣኔዎስ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ውስቴትኪ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ኦግብጽ ፡ ወበውስተ ፡ ግብጻዊያን ፡ ወበኀቤሆሙ ። ወስብሐት ፡ "ለእግዚእነ"የ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወለአቡሁ ፡ ዔር ፡ ወለመንፈሱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡
they remained in a cave which is now in the church of the saint Sargiyos (= Sergius) in Mesr. After that, they went out of Mesr and arrived at Mataryä; they bathed there and the spring of water became holy and blessed from that moment. Our Lord had made it gush forth as we (have said before). There comes out of it an oil from the balsam that our Lord planted, with which are completed Christian baptism and the consecration of churches, altars, and sacred vessels. By it one performs remissions and healing for all illnesses. It is given as a gift to kings and they glory in it. From there, they went to Mehdäb (= El-Mahmeh). By his (= Jesus') return was fulfilled the prophecy of 'Ozyä (= Hosea) the prophet saying: "Out of Egypt I have called my Son." It is fitting that we celebrate a spiritual feast on this day and that we sing in it as says Däwit (= David) the prophet: God performed miracles in Egypt and wonders in the fields of Tänëwos (= Tanis). And also: He performed in your midst, Egypt, wonders, in the midst of the Egyptians and among them. Glory to our Lord Jesus Christ, to his excellent Father, and to his life-giving Holy Spirit for ever and ever, amen!
ማሕየዊ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ እንባቆም ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘእም! ፲ወ፪ደቂቀ ፡ ነቢያት" ። ዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አብሰለ ፡ ብርስነ ፡ ጸብሕ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይወስድ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እለ ፡ የአርሩ ፡ እክለ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ኅብስት ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ውስድ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መብልዓ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግበ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ለባቢሎንሰ” ፡ ኢርኢክዋ ፡ ወግበሰ ፡ ኢየአምር ። ወአኀዞ ፣ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በሥዕርተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ወዘይጸውር” ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መብልፅ ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ውስተ” ፡ ግበ" ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሕቱም ፡ ውእቱ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መብልዓ ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወበልዓ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ሜጦ ፣ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወልህቀ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እምፃፄዋዌ ፡ ወሐነጹ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ቦአ ፡ እንባቆም ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወተቀበልዎ ፡ በፍሥሐ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወአሰርገዉ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ወተጋብኡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስምዑ ፡ ትንቢቶ ። ወከሠተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ድምፀከ ፡ ወፈራህኩ ። ርኢኩ ፡ ግብረከ ፡ ወአንከርኩ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተናገረ ፡ በትንቢቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ወበእንተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ በቤተ ፤ ልሔም ፡ ዘይሁዳ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እምደብረ ፡ ፋራን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነበበ ፡ ትንቢቶ” ፡ እስከ ፣ ተፍጻሜቱ ። ወጸሐፍዋ ፡ ወደመርዋ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ትንቢታተ" ፡ ነቢያት ። ወኃደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወመጽአት ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትበኪ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሀለዉኒ ፡ ፪ውሉድ ፡ ኃሠሥዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምልኩ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ ወአበዩ ፡ ወቀተልዎሙ ፡ ወገደፍዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ። ወወፅአ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ኅበ ፡
On this day also died 'Enbägom (= Habakkuk) the prophet who (is among) the twelve minor prophets. One day, this prophet had cooked a lentil stew and (was) bringing it to those who were harvesting the wheat; (he had) bread with him. The angel of God appeared to him and said to him: "Bring this food to Dän'ël (= Daniel) the prophet in the lions' den in the city of Bäbilon (= Babylon)." He said to him: "My Lord, Bäbilon, I have (never) seen it, and I do not know the den." The angel of God seized him by the hair, as well as the food he was carrying, and brought him into the den of the city of Bäbilon while (the latter) was sealed. He gave the food to Dän'êl the prophet (who) ate. And, on the spot, the angel of God brought him back to the land of Yehudä (= Judah). His days lengthened greatly. When the children of Israel returned from captivity and (re)built the Temple, 'Enbägom entered into Jerusalem; they welcomed him with great joy, decorated the Temple, and gathered to hear his prophecy. He opened his mouth by the Holy Spirit and said: "Lord, I have heard your noise and I have been afraid; I have seen your work and I have admired." Then he spoke in his prophecy of the incarnation of our Lord Christ and of his birth in Bêta lehëm (= Bethlehem) of Yehuda; he said: God will come from the mountain of Farän. Then he pronounced his prophecy until the end. It was written and joined to the prophecies of the prophets. He remained in Jerusalem. A woman from among the children of Israel came to him weeping and said to him: "I had two sons, they were asked

to worship idols; they refused, they were killed, and they were thrown on the road." He went out with her to where her killed sons were, he asked God to make their soul return into them, and God welcomed his prayer and made her (= the woman's) two sons live again. When the moment of his death was near, he called his relatives and told them that he was going to die. He remained an hour looking upward and behold, a great arm, like a man's hand, opened the roof of the house, descended from above, reached toward his mouth, and received his soul. When it was the time of 'Anestäsyos (= Anastasius) the Christian emperor, when he read his (= the saint's) story, he (had) a church built for him in the city of Qartäs (= Qamatsä) in Lower Egypt and it was consecrated on the same (day) as this day. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. [On this day also became a martyr the blessed and chosen martyr, the (spiritual) combatant 'Abqueltä. This saint was from a family of notables of the city of ‘Ensenä (= Antinoe). He was a pure priest and a learned physician. He did good to all the sick who came to him and he treated them without pay. Moreover, he gave them food, drink, clothes, and everything they needed. In those days, Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) the impious emperor sent into the regions of the South a governor by the name of ‘Aryänos (= Arianus) so that he might force the people of this country to worship idols. Then they took this saint from 'Ensenä, led him to the city of 'Esmunäyn (= Eschmounein), and made him stand before the governor. The governor spoke to him in a soothing voice to turn him away from his right faith, but he could (do nothing) about this. Then he ordered to torture him with different tortures until his brain liquefied. Then they burned him in the fire while he was alive. Then he finished his martyrdom on the twenty-fourth of Genbot. Believers came, shrouded him beautifully, and buried him in a mountain of the north (of Egypt) near his city. May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]
አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአልዓዛር ፡ ካህን ፡ ወልደ ፡ አሮን ፡ ወተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአክሊድስ ፡ ወቴዎፍላስ"' ፡ ወለበዐለ ፡፣ ማርዩ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of ‘Al'äzär (= Eleazar) the priest son of ‘Aron (= Aaron) and the commemoration of 'Aklides, Téwofläs (= Theophilus), and of Ba'ala Märyu. May God have mercy through their prayer upon their dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 25TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ፳፳ለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፤ አእረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡፤ ሰሎሜ ፡ እኅታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ። ወይእቲ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ማጣት ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሌዊ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. [On this day died the holy Salomë (= Salome), the sister of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary Mother of God. She (is) the daughter of Mätat (= Matthat) son

of Lëwi (= Levi), son of Melki (= Melchi), son of 'Aron (= Aaron) the priest. Mätat had three daughters; the name of one, the oldest, (was) Märyam (= Mary), (that) of the second, Sofyä (= Sophia), (that) of the third, Hannä (= Anne). This Märyäm gave birth to Salomë, Sofyä gave birth to 'Élsäbet (= Elizabeth), and Hannä gave birth to our Lady the holy Virgin Mary. This holy Salomë had been raised in the house of Yosëf (= Joseph) with our Lady the holy Virgin Mary. She remained serving her and never separated from her. When our Savior Jesus Christ was born, Salomë was present with her. She saw a great light that shone at the moment of the birth and the soldiers of heaven ascending and descending in an immense, ineffable glory; they were worshipping the coming of the Shepherd in the stable. When Yosëf had doubts about our Lady the holy Virgin Mary as to how she had given birth while she was a virgin, this holy Salomë dared to touch the seal of her virginity so much so that her fingers burned from the fire of the divinity. When she touched the child, she was healed (and became) as before. By this Yosëf knew that our Lady the holy Virgin Mary was the Mother of God. When Hërodes (= Herod) ordered to kill the little children of Bêta lehëm (= Bethlehem), she made the child flee with Yosëf and his mother our Lady Mary and associated herself with all his weakness: sometimes she carried him (in her arms), sometimes she fed him. She served him during all the days of his (= Jesus') life—thirty-three years. On the day of his passion also, she did not separate from him, lamenting and weeping. On the day of his resurrection also, she contemplated him before the Apostles. In the upper room of Seyon (= Sion), the Holy Spirit descended upon her with her (relatives?), the holy women. She made many (people) believe in the faith of our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him!—and encountered from the Jews much folly and hostility. Then she died in peace. May God have mercy upon her dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

On this day, there is the holy Kolotos (= Coluthus) martyr, the physician who (was) from the city of 'Andënäaw (= Antinoe). The father of this saint feared God and, likewise, his mother also. His father was governor of the city of 'Andënäw. He had no son. He constantly implored our Lord Christ so much so that he gave him this saint. He educated him and taught him to write. (The child) knew by heart the books of the Church. He was pure from his childhood, devout and ascetic. He said many prayers. The rule of his prayer was, at night, one hundred prayers and likewise during the day. When he had grown a little, his parents sought to marry him, but he did not want that. They had a daughter whom they had begotten after the saint Kolotos. They married her to 'Aryänos (= Arianus), the governor of 'Andënäw who became governor after the father of this saint. Indeed when his days had grown, the father of this saint addressed

ወሠዓሮ ፡ እምሚመት" ፡ ወመጠዎ” ፡ ሚመተ ፡ ለአርያኖስ ፡ ሐሙሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ሐነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ነግድ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፈላሲያን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተምህረ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ጠቢበ ። ወኩሉ ፤ ድውያን ፡ ይበጽሑ'" ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይፌውሶሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዓስብ ። ወሶበ ፡ ክሕደ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ወተሰነአወ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ በክሕደቱ ፡ በእንተ ፤ ሚመቱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይቤንኖሙ!፤ ፡ ለሰማዕታት" ። ወፈቀደ” ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮሎጦስ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዐውደ ፡ ምኩናን ፡ ወረገሞ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡፤ ወለአርያኖስ ፤ ሐሙሔ"! ፡ ወለጣዖታቲሆሙ” ። ወኢተክህሎ ፡ ለአርያኖስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምእኩይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ አላ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ” ፡ ብህንሳ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፤ ሞቅሕ ፤ ይመዓልተ”" ፡ ወፈነወት ፡ እኅቱ ፣ ወአድኃነቶ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ካልእ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአይድፅዎ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ኮሎጦስ”" ። ወፈነወ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ወተቁጥዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢወገርከ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ለአማልክት ፡ ወእመአኮ ፡ አነ ፡ እቤንነከ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይሰግድ”' ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ ርኩሳን ፡ አላ ፡ አነ ፡፤ ዕወግር ፡ ለእግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወኩነንዎ” ፡ በዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ኩነኔ ። ወኮነ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወይናዝዞ ፡ ወያጸንዖ ፡ ወያስተዔግሦ ፡ ወይፌውስ ፡ ቀ-ስላቲሁ” ። ወገብረ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ዓበይተ”! ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፤ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ኢተመይጠ ፤ አምክሩ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኮሎጦስ”፡ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ገነዝዎ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ብዙኃት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ይምሐረነ ፡፤ በጸሎቱ ፡
the emperor and (the latter) dismissed him from (his) office and gave the office to 'Aryänos his son-in-law. When his parents died, (the saint) built a hostel for travelers. Then he learned the books of medicine and became a physician. All the sick came to him and he treated them without pay. When Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) apostatized, 'Aryänos agreed with him in his apostasy because of his office, and he began to torment the martyrs. This saint Kolotos wanted to become a martyr, he went to the tribunal and cursed the emperor, 'Aryänos his brother-in-law, and their idols. 'Aryänos could do nothing evil against him because of his sister, but he sent him to the city of Behnesä (= Behnesah) and (the saint) remained three days in prison. His sister sent and delivered him from the prison. After 'Aryänos, another governor was appointed. He was told the story of this saint Kolotos. He sent, had him come, became furious against him, and said to him: "If you do not offer incense to the gods, I will make you undergo a great torture." The saint said to him: "I do not prostrate myself before impure idols, but I offer (incense) to my Lord Jesus Christ Son of the living God." Then the governor became irritated and ordered to torture the saint, and they tortured him with different tortures. The angel of God came to him all the time, consoled him, strengthened him, exhorted him to constancy, and healed his wounds. God performed great miracles by his hands. When the governor was tired of torturing him—and he (= the saint) did not turn away from his sentiment—he ordered to cut off his head; they cut off the head of the saint Kolotos with the sword and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Then the people of his house shrouded him and placed him in a beautiful place. Miracles and wonders appeared from his body. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen.
ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ” ፣፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ አባ ፡ ሂሩዳ” ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ሰምስጣ” ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እምኀበ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሐዲ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሂሩዳ” ፡ ይፈርሆ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እምንእሱ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ይሰክብ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዓረቱ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይሔሊ ፡ በልቡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሥምረቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ምንት ፡ ዘይበቀዓኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንበር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ። እስመ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ዘኢይክሕድ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዓለመ ፡ ወዘውስቴቱ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ ይጸመደኒ ። ወይእዚዜኒ ፡ እትነሣእ ፡ ወአሐውር ፡ ወእክፅው ፡ ደምየ ፡ በስሙ” ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይሔሊ ፡
On this day also became a martyr the great saint abbä Hirudä (= Herod?). This saint was from the city of Samestä and (lived) when it was the days of the persecution (waged) against the people of the Christians by Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) the apostate. This saint abbä Hirudä feared God from his childhood. While he was lying on a bed one day—he was thinking in his heart about the will of God—he said in his heart: "What does it profit me to dwell in this transitory world? I have heard the voice of my Lord Jesus Christ who says in the holy Gospel: ‘Whoever does not renounce this world and what (is) in it cannot join me.’ Now (it is necessary) that I get up, go, and shed my blood for the name of Christ." While he was thinking about this in his heart, behold, Mikä'el (= Michael) the archangel came to him and

ዘንተ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦቅዱስ ፡ ወብፁዕ ፡ አባ ፡ ሂሩዳ" ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አስተዳለወ ፡ ለከ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ወመንበረ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ሰማያዊት" ። አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ አነ ፡ አሐውር ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካነ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወኢይትቴጌየል” ፡ ላዕለ ፤ ሥጋከ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ እምኩነኔ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወብፁዕሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ሂሩዳ”" ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምዕራበ” ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወሜጠ ፡ገጾ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እስአለከ ፡ ኦአብ ፡ አኃዜ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ወወልድከ" ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'ዘኩሉ ፡ በረከት ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ወሎቱ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ስብሐት” ። እስእለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታጽንዓኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ እፈጽም ፡ ዘአብፃዕኩ ፡ ለከ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዓውደ ፡ ምኩናን ፡ ወጸርኃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ 'ገሃደ ፡ ወይቤሉ”' ፡ መኩንን ፡ እምአይቴ” ፡ አንተ ፡ ወመኑ ፡ ስምከ” ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሂሩዳ፤ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰምስጣ” ፡ እምደወለ ፡ ብህንሳ ። ወእምንእስየ ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መልእክቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ሉኪያኖስ” ፡ ተደለው ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሥዕ ፡ ለአማልክት ፡ 'አብሎን ፤ አርጣሚስ ፤ ከመ ፡ አሌዕለከ ፡ ወአከብረከ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ወእሁበከ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሂሩዳ” ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአብድ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ አሌሎቱ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ዘቱክልቱ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወብፁዕ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘቱክልቱ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ በአምላከ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወአንሰ ፡ ኢይሠውዕ ፡ ለጣዖታቲከ ፡ ርኩሳት ፡ እስመ ፡ ተስፋየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ 'እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅስፍዎ” ፡ ወቀሠፍዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ 'በአብትርት ፡ ዘበቀልት” ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሦኩ ፡ እስከ ፡ ውሕዘ ፡ ደሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማይ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ኢሐመ ፣ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር"! ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወገሠሰ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ” ፡ ወፈወሶ ፡ እምቀ“ስላቲሁ” ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ወጐልቆሙ ፡ ፳፻ዕደው” ።
said to him: "Greetings to you, holy and blessed abbä Hirudä. Do not be afraid, behold our Lord Jesus Christ has prepared for you a crown and a throne of glory in his heavenly kingdom. I am Mikä'el the archangel, I will go with you to the place of torture and no torture will prevail over your holy body." Having said this, Mikä'ël the archangel gave him the greeting and ascended to heaven. As for the blessed abbä Hirudä, he went out toward the west of his city to some (distance), turned his face toward the east, and prayed thus saying: "I implore you, almighty Father, as well as your only Son Jesus Christ from whom (comes) and in whom (is) all blessing—to him belongs the glory! I implore you to strengthen me until I have accomplished that which I have vowed to you." Having said this, he went to the tribunal and cried out saying: "I am a Christian openly!" The governor said to him: "Where are you from and what is your name?" The saint abbä Hirudä answered him and said to him: "I am from the people of the city of Samestä of the district of Behnesä (= Behnesah). Since my youth, I have remained a soldier in the service of the emperor." The governor Lukiyänos (= Lucianus) said to him: "Prepare yourself to sacrifice to the gods 'Ablon (= Apollon) (and) 'Artämis (= Artemis) so that I will raise you and honor you more than all the soldiers, and I will give you much money." Abbä Hirudä answered him and said to him: "Fool, (it is) written in the holy Scriptures (words) which say: Woe to the man whose trust (is) in man, happy the man whose trust (is) in the Lord, in the God of heaven. As for me, I will not sacrifice to your impure idols, for my Lord Jesus Christ is my hope." Then the governor became irritated and ordered to scourge him. They scourged the saint with thorny palm branches until his blood flowed like water. But the saint did not suffer at all from this torture, for God was with him. Immediately Mika'ël the archangel came from heaven to him; he touched the body of the saint and healed him of his wounds. When the people saw this miracle, they believed in our Lord Jesus Christ and became martyrs. Their number (is) five hundred men. When the governor was tired of torturing him, he ordered to cut off his head with the sword. At that moment, soldiers came and put a bit in the saint's mouth.
ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ" ፡ በሰይፍ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ጊዜ ፡'መጽኡ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ወለጐምዎ" ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ በልጓም ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉሁ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይወስድዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ" ፡ ወናሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦብፁዕ ፡ አባ ፡ ሂሩዳ” ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አምላክከ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አስተዳለውኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በሰማያት ። አማን” ፡ እብለከ ፡ ኩሉ” ፡ ዘይዘክር ፡ ጸማከ ፡ ወይገብር ፡ ፉርርባነ ፡ ወይሁብ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ተዝካርከ ፡ ኢይኩን'፤ ፡ ሎቱ” ፡
While they were leading him to cut off his head, behold, our Lord Jesus Christ appeared to him and said to him: "Greetings to you, blessed abbä Hirudä, I am Christ your God. Do not be afraid, behold I have prepared for you a throne with the saints in the heavens. In truth I tell you: whoever will mention your pain, will make a sacrifice, and will give alms to the poor on the


[p. 330] {#po.47.p330}
ውስተ ፡ እንስሳሁ ፡ መካን ፣ ወኢይሕጣእ” ፡ እምቤቱ ፡፤ ውሉደ ፡ ወኢይክል” ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ግሙራ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ምግባራቲሁ ። አው ፡ ዘይጽሕፍ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፤ ገድልከ ፡ ወስምዕከ ፡ አነ ፡ እጽሕፍ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ወእደመስስ ፡ ሎቱ” ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ዕዳሁ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወይዘክር ፡ ስመከ ፡ አነ ፡ ኣድኅኖ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ምንዳቤሁ ። 'ወኩሉ ፡ ዘይገብር” ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ አነ ፡ አኃድግ ። ሎቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አበሳሁ ። ሰላምየ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ እግዚእነ” ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት” ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ሂሩዳ” ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፤ ዓቢየ ፤ ፍሥሐ ፡ በተፍጻሜተ ፡ ተጋድሎቱ ። ወተመይጠ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ አለ ፡ ይተልውዎ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፤ ለአመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምውስቴትክሙ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይፈርሆ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ለይንሣእ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ወያንብሮ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ በረከት ፡ ዓቢይ ፤ ትነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘያነብሩዎ ፣ ሥጋየ ፣፤ ውስቴቱ! ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ ክሳዶ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ቅድስተ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወ፳ለወርኃ ፡ ግንቦት ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኒአውራኅ ፡ መጽኡ ፡፤ አዝማዲሁ ፡ ወጾሩ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወአብጽሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ሰምሰጣ” ፡ ወአንበረዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘሐነጹ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ወመንክራት ፡ወፈውስ ፡ ለድውያን ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወትንብልናሁ ፡
day of your commemoration, for him may there not be among his beasts any barren one, may he not lack sons in his house, and Satan will never be able to do anything against him in all his actions. Or (also), whoever will write the book of your life and of your martyrdom, (for him) I will write his name in the book of life and I will erase for him the book of his debt. Whoever will be in peril and will mention your name, (him), I will save from all his peril. Whoever will make your commemoration, to him I will remit all his faults. May my peace be with you." Having said this, our Lord ascended into the heavens in a great glory. As for the saint abbä Hirudä, he experienced a great joy at the end of his (spiritual) combat. He turned toward the people who were following him and said to them: "If there is among you a man who fears God, let him take my body and place it near him, for a great blessing will dwell in the place where they will place my body." Then he stretched out his neck, they cut off his holy head with the sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven on the twenty-fifth of the month of Genbot. Seven months later, his relatives came, took the body of the saint, brought it to his city, Samsatä, and placed it in a place they had built for him. Miracles and wonders occurred from it and healing for the sick. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us for ever and ever, amen.
የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ 'ኮነ ፤ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳንዎ ፡ ባላኖስ ፡ ወእሎንትራን” ፡ ወራምኒሳ ፡ ወ፪፻ወዛሞ ሰማዕታት ፣ ወቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ሄላ ፡ ወበዘተከለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ አብትረ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ በቢሶስ ፡ መካን ፡ 'ወኮኑ ፡ አዕዋመ”' ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day (also), there is the death of the saints Bälänos, 'Elonterän (= Elantherius), Rämnisä, (of) two hundred and fifty martyrs, (of) Quastantinos (= Constantine) the emperor and (of) his mother Hëlä (= Helena), while our Lord planted the staffs of Yosëf (= Joseph) in the place (called) Bisos, and 115 became trees. May God have mercy through their prayer upon their dear 'Atnätëwos in the ages of ages, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 26TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ፳ፄ8ለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ'፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ሰማዕት" ፡ ዘይሰመይ ፡ ዲዲሞስ" ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሰበከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ህንደኬ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ውስተ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ህንደኬ ፡ረሰየ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ለ8ብእሲ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሉኪዮስ' ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዓርኩ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወተስእሎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንግረኒ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዘተአምር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አነ ፡ ነዳቂ ፡ አሐንጽ ፡ ምኩራባተ ፡ ወአጽርሕተ ፡ ወአነ ፡ ጸራቢ ፡ ወእጸርብ ፣ መዋፍረ ፡ ወመናብርተ ፡ ወካልኣነ ፡ ወአነ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ወእፌውሶሙ ፡ ለድውያን ፡ እለ ፡ ቄሰሉ ፡ በደዌ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ነገሮ ፡ ዓደሞ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ 'ዝንቱ ፡ ገብር" ፡ ዘይበቀዕ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወበውእቱ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became a martyr the holy Tomäs (= Thomas) the Apostle who is called Didimos (= Didymus) after having preached in the cities of Hendakë (= India). This (took place) thus: when the saint entered the cities of Hendakë, he made himself the servant of a man by the name of Lukiyos (= Lucius). This man was a friend of the king; he questioned the saint and said to him: "Tell me what work you know." The saint said to him: "I am a builder (and) I build temples and palaces; I am a carpenter and I make plows, chairs, and other

(objects); I am a physician and I heal the sick who suffer from illness." When his master heard his speech, it pleased him much and he said: "This servant (is) someone who will be useful to the king." At that moment, the man went out to go to the king and he left the saint Tomäs in his house. The saint began to teach his mistress the way of God, to prescribe to her to walk in purity and to make her understand the mysteries of the Son of God. She believed in his word and, likewise, many of the people of the house. When his master returned from the king and saw what Tomäs the Apostle had done, he said to him: "Evil servant, where are the beautiful works that you told me you would do?" The saint answered him and said to him: "Lord, I did not lie: the temples and the palaces that I have built are the souls of those who have become a dwelling for the King of glory. The plows that I have made are the holy Gospels which have harrowed and uprooted the thorns and thistles which (are) of sin. As for the medicine and the remedies, they are the holy Mysteries, the holy body and the glorious blood of our Lord Jesus Christ which heal from the deadly poison which (is) of sin." When he heard this from him, (his master) ordered the saint to be laid on the ground, his hands and feet to be tied with four ropes, to be stretched, the ropes to be tied to four stakes, his skin to be flayed, and his body to be coated with salt, vinegar, and lime. They did this to him as he (had) ordered. As for him (= the saint), (he endured this) with a constancy (that came) from God. When his mistress saw him from the window of her house while they were flaying his skin, she fell immediately and gave up the ghost. When Lukiyos her husband knew that his wife was dead, he was greatly afflicted. As for the saint Tomäs the Apostle, God refreshed his wounds and healed his body. Lukiyos said to him: "Behold, my wife is dead because of you. If you resurrect her from the dead, I will believe in your God." Tomäs the Apostle went in to her and placed his skin upon her, saying: "Arsonewä (= Arsenia), in the name of our Lord Jesus Christ, arise." Immediately she opened her eyes, rose, stood up, and prostrated herself before Tomäs the Apostle. When Lukiyäs saw this, he believed in our Lord Jesus Christ, and, likewise, all the people of his house and all the people of the city believed in our Lord Jesus Christ. (Tomäs) baptized them in the name of the holy Trinity, consecrated for them a bishop and priests, and built for them a church. He remained with them thirty days, strengthening them in the right faith. Whoever had an illness would put his (= Tomäs') skin upon him and was healed immediately.
ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ዘቦ ፡ደዌ ፡ያነብር ፡ አነዳሁ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ወይትፌወስ” ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወጽአ” ፡ እምኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቆንጦርያ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይበውእ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ረከበ ፡ ፳ብእሴ ፡ አረጋዌ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ” ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትበኪ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ወአውሥኦ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሀለዉኒ ፡ ኗውሉድ ፡ ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ንዋይ ። ወላዕሌየ ፡ ፅዳ ፡ ለካልኣን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወአልብየ ፡ ዘእፈድዮሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ተረፈኒ ፤ ፳8እምኔሆሙ ፡ እምኮነ ፡
He departed from them and entered the city of Qontoryä. As he was entering it, he met an old man shedding bitter tears. The saint said to him: "Why are you weeping thus?" (The old man) answered him and said to him: "I had seven sons; the king killed them for money. And I have a debt to other people and have nothing with which to repay them." || he said again: "If there remained to me

one of them, he would give me help for my life." The saint gave him his skin and said to him: "Put this upon your sons." When he had done as the saint had prescribed to him, his seven sons rose immediately. This miracle spread throughout the whole city. The priests of the idols became furious because of this and threw stones at the saint Tomäs the Apostle. Their hands withered; they all believed in our Lord Christ and (the Apostle) baptized them with Christian baptism and taught them the law of the Church. After that, he went to the city of Kinäs and to the city of Magëdonyä and preached there the name of our Lord Jesus Christ. The kings and the governors heard (about) him, had him seized, and imprisoned him. The wife of a king and many (people) with her came to him in secret; he taught them the way of God and many believed through his word. The king became very angry because of his wife, but he could not kill him (= Tomäs) in the midst of the people. He had him taken outside the city and ordered four soldiers to pierce him with spears, and they pierced him with spears. The king's son was standing (there) while they were piercing him with spears until he gave up the ghost. When the people of the city knew (this), they went out to deliver him from the hands of the soldiers, but they found that he had given up the ghost. They carried away the body of the saint, shrouded it, and placed it in a tomb among the tombs of the kings. As for the king's son, Satan took hold of him and made him roll (on the ground). They came to the body of the saint Tomäs to take a little of his garment and hang it over the king's son. When they opened the tomb, they did not find the body of the saint, for God had taken it away. They took some dust from the tomb with faith and hung it over the king's son: he was healed immediately. The saint Tomäs appeared to many people of the city and taught them that he was alive and that our Lord Christ had welcomed him. He prescribed to them to be firm in the faith in the name of our Lord Christ.
ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። "ወእምተአምሪሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ዘአስተርአዩ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ደሴት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ህንደኬ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፳ቀሲስ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ወይፈርሆ” ፡ ለእግዚአ ብሔር ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ”፤ ፡ ህንደኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግድ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፤ ፳ወ፳ለግንቦት ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ሌሊተ” ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ተጋቢኦሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ዘውስቴቱ ፡ ደሴት ፡ ኀበ ፡ሀሎ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ኮነ ፡ ነፋሰ ፡ አውሎ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ እሰከ ፡ ኃልቀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ። ወሖሩ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ደሴት ፡ ወረከብዎ ፡ ለዓቃቤ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘአዕረፈ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወገነዝዎ ፡ ወቀበርዎ ። ወጸለዩ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ኩለንታሃ ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወፄለግንቦት ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ቶማስ ፡፤ ቀደሱ ፡ ዮርባነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ወጸሎተ ፡ ፈትቶ ፡ አንበሩ ፡
Among the miracles of this saint Tomäs which appeared in the island in the land of Hendakë, (there is) this one: a just priest—and he feared God—from the land of Egypt went to the land of Hendakë to travel. When it was the twenty-fifth of Genbot, that is to say the night of the feast of the saint Tomäs the Apostle, he saw many people gathered at the edge of the sea in which (was) the island where the hand of the saint Tomäs was. At that moment, there was a violent storm wind to the point that the water disappeared. All the people went and entered the church of the saint Tomäs which (was) on the island. They found the guardian of the church who was dead at that moment; they shrouded him and buried him. They prayed the entire night. The next day, that is to say the 26th of Genbot, the feast of the saint Tomäs, they consecrated the Eucharist. When the Eucharist and the prayer

ሥጋሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሕያውታ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። ወመጽኡ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ለለ፳፳” ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመጠዉ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ እምእዴሁ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይትሜጠዉ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ፳ብእሲ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሕዝብ?" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመጠው ፡ ወጸበጠ ፡፤ እራኃ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወከልሑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ኪርያላይሶን ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ነዊሃ ። ወተከሥተ ፡ እራሑ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወተመጠወ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡፤ ወከማሁ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፣ ተመጠዉ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአተዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ በሰላም ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ነፋሰ ፡ ዓቢየ” ፡ ወሜጦ ፡ ለማየ ፡ ባሕር ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ለለ ፡ ዓመት ፡ እስከ ፡ ዮም” ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ቶማስ ፡
of the fraction (of the bread) were finished, they placed the holy body of our Lord in the hand of the saint Tomäs the Apostle, this hand of his being alive (and) without corruption. The people came one by one to receive the holy Mysteries from his hand. While they were receiving (them), a man among the people came to receive (them) and the palm of his (= Tomäs') hand closed over the holy body. All the people cried out saying "Kyrie eleison!" many times and they made a long prayer. The palm of the saint opened and the man received the holy Mysteries and, likewise, all the people received the holy Mysteries, went out of the church, and returned to their houses in peace. At that moment, God—glory to him!—sent a violent wind (which) put the water of the sea back as it was before. The same thing happens every year until today. May the prayer of this blessed saint apostle Tomäs, his intercession, and his blessing be with us in the ages of ages, amen.

[On this day also, there is 'Elfätus the martyr and (the commemoration) of Zakäryäs (= Zechariah) the solitary and of the saint 'Arsonyä (= Arsenia), the wife of Lukyos (= Lucius). May God have mercy through their prayer upon their dear 'Atnätëwos in the ages of ages.]

### READING FOR THE 27TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ፳ጂለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ' ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ክርስቲያናዌ" ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሞቅሐ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ዖማዕት ፡ ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ በአእምሮ ፡፤ ወጊሩት ። ወአኀዝዎ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወደረሰ ፤ በመዋዕለ ፤ ሚመቱ ፡ ድርሳናተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ። ወአቆመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቀርነ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ። እስመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አንስጣስዮስ ፡ መሃይምን ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፤ ሳዊሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳዊሮስ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ በሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ እስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አምላክነ ፡ እምድኅረ ፤ ተዋሕዶቱ ፡ አሐቲ ፡፤ ጠባይዓት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ፍልጠት" ፡ በከመ ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ ለአበዊነ ፡ አባ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ' ። ወተወክፈ ፡ መልእክቶ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆልሳተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ አእኩትዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔረ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተመይጦተ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father abbä Yohannes (= John), patriarch of the city of Alexandria. This saint was a Christian; he became a monk from his childhood and led a great (spiritual) combat. Then he shut himself in a cell and his reputation for knowledge and virtue spread. They seized him and consecrated him patriarch of the city of Alexandria. During the days of his office, he composed numerous homilies. God strengthened the power of the Church during the days of this father, for the emperor in those days was 'Anestäsyos (= Anastasius), the just believer, and the holy father abbä Säwiros (= Severus) was patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch). Abbä Säwiros wrote a letter to this father abbä Yohannes on the right faith, saying in it: "Christ our God (is) after his union one single nature (= tabäy'ät) without separation, according to the faith of our fathers abbä Qérellos (= Cyril) and abbä Diyosqoros (= Dioscorus)." Abbä Yohannes accepted his letter as well as all his bishops; they praised God and glorified him because of the coming together of the members who had been separated and were (from then on) returned to their place. Then abbä Yohannes wrote him a reply to his letter, in terms full of the grace of the right faith, on the union of the divinity of God and his three hypostases (= helläwë), on the incarnation

መልያልያት ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ተሌለዩ ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኖሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ተሰጥዎ ፣ መልእክቱ ፡ በቃላት ፡ ምሉኣት ፡ እምጸጋ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተዋሕዶተ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወ፻ህላዌሁ ። ወበእንተ ፡ ተሠግዎቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በጠባይዓተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ኮነት ፡'አሐደ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መለኮት" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ፍልጠት ፤ ወኢትድምርት'፤! ፡ ወኢውላጤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ወኢኮነ" ፡ ክልኤተ ። ወያወግዝ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይፈልጥዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ አው ፡ ይቶስሑ ፡ ጠባይዓቶ ። ወአውገዞሙ ፡ 'ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ" ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ከመዝ” ፡ ሐመ ፡ ወተሰቅለ ፤ ወሞተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ፅራቁ ፡ አው ፡ ያበውእ ፡ ሕማመ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ጠባይዓተ ፡ መለኮት ። አላ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕትሰ ፡ እስመ” ፡ ነአምን ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቃል ፡ ሐመ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ እምኔነ ። ወዛቲ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ዘኢይስሕት ፡ ወኢይትዓቀፍ ፡ ዘየሐውር ፡ ውስቴታ ። ወሶበ ፡ አንበበ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳዊሮስ ፡ መልእክቶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ተወክፈ” ፣ በሠናይ ፡ ተወክፎ ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወተሰናአው ፡ በማእከለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ 'አንጾኪያ ፡ ወበማእከለ ፡፣ ሀገረ'' ፡
of the Son of God in human nature and on the way in which he became one with the divinity without separation nor mixture nor change, and (how) he became one and did not become two. || anathematized all those who separated Christ or mixed his natures. || anathematized all those who said thus: "(He who) suffered, was crucified, and died for humanity (is) a simple man," or (he who) introduces pain into the divine nature. "But, (he said,) as for the right faith, we believe that God the Word suffered for us in the flesh that he took from us. This is the way of the (heavenly) kingdom on which he who travels it neither errs nor stumbles." When abbä Säwiros read the letter of this father abbä Yohannes, he gave it a beautiful welcome and preached it in the city of 'Ansokiyä. And there was peace and concord between the city of 'Ansokiyä and the city of Alexandria in the right faith. This father remained eleven years teaching his flock, exhorting it, watching over it, and strengthening it in the right faith, and he died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
እስክንድርያ ፡ በሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። ወነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ፲ወ፳ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ለመርዔቱ” ፡ ወይጌሥጾሙ ፡ ወያዓቅቦሙ ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ በሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አልዓዛር” ፡ እጉሆን ፡ ለማርታ ፡ ወለማርያ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቆብሮስ” ። እስመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ አንሥኦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፤ እሙታን ፡ ወሐመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ሱባኤ ፡ ተለዎሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ አንበሩ”፤ ፡ አዴሆሙ” ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቆብሮስ” ፡ ወዓቀበ ፣ መርዔቶ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ። ወነበረ ፡ በሚመቱ ፡ ጓጣዓመተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡፤ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡፤ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን።
On this day also died the saint 'Al'azär (= Lazarus), the brother of Märtä (= Martha) and of Märyä (= Mary), after he had been consecrated bishop of the city of Qobros (= Cyprus). Indeed, after our Lord had resurrected him from the dead and our Lord had suffered during (Holy) Week, this saint followed the Apostles from that moment. After the Holy Spirit the Paraclete had descended upon them, they laid hands on him and consecrated him bishop of the city of Qobros, and he guarded his flock with a beautiful guard. He remained in his office forty years and died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብረ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ዘይብልዎ ፡ ዳጋ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day (also) died Gabra Mikä'ël the governor and he was buried in the cemetery of the just which is called Dägä. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.

### READING FOR THE 28TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ፳ቿለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አባ ፡ ጌርሎስ ፡ ወጓወ፳ደቂቁ ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ክቡራን ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. [On this day became martyrs abbä Gêrellos (= Cyril) and also his forty-five children, and (there is) also the commemoration of the illustrious fathers 'Abrehäm (= Abraham), Yeshaq (= Isaac), and Yä'qob (= Jacob). May their holy blessing be with their dear 'Atnätéwos in the ages of ages, amen.]

ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ መርቆሬዎስ' ፡ ዘእምኔተ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ። ይቤ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ፍልሰቱ ፡ ለእኅው ፡ መርቆሬዎስ ፡ ወሖርኩ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እንሣእ ፡ በረከቶ ። ወረከብክዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምሠጥ ፡ ወኢተፈልጠት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ። ወነበርነ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃው ፡ ሠሉሰ ፡ ለያልየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢንጥዕም ፡ እክለ ፡ ወበሣልስት ፡ ዕለት ፡ ገብአት ፣ እስትንፋሱ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ 'ርእየኒ ፡ ይቤለኒ" ፡ ምንት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተጋብኦትክሙ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ወነገርኖ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ወሰአልኖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዜንወነ ፡ ዘርእየ ። ወነገረነ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ግሩም ። ወነሥአ ፤ መንፈሶ ፡ ወአርአዮ ፡ ሥቃየ ፡ ኃጥኣን ፡ ወዕሜተ ፡ ጻድቃን ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስቆቀወ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ወወረወ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወይቤለነ ፡ አኃውየ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደሩ ፡ ለአባ" ፡ ጊዮርጊሰ ፡ ዘገዳመ ፡ ሐራጺት ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰማዕክዎ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ዘምስሌየ ፡ እንዘ ፡፣ ይብል ፡ ሚጥዎ ፡ ለመርቆሬዎስ' ፡ ወአምጽእዎ ፡ ለጊዮርጊስ ። 'ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ" ፡ ነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐት ፡ ንስቲት” ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ፄው ፡ እስከ ፡ ተቄረጸ'" ፡ ሥጋሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ አኃው ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ ምውተ ፡ ወአምጽእዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ምኔት ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ህየ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ እኅው'! ፡ መርቆሬዎስ'፤ ፡ አጸመወ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ይቡስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተሰነአለኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወ8ለነሐሰሴ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ትረክበኒ ። ወበዕለተ ፡ አደመኒ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወረከብክዎ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ አዓርፍ ፡ ወአሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ አበውየ ፡ ወይመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤክ ፡ ዞቶራት ፡ ወአጹር"' ፡ ሣጋየ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዘባናቲሆሙ ፡ ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ይመርሔከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምኔትከ ፡ በፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወድፍነኒ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ማኅደርየ ። ወኮነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ወተከሥተ ፡ እመቃብሪሁ ፡ ተአምራት ፡፤ ወመንክራት ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጐልዋኑ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ አመተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ተጋዳሊት ፡ መናኒተ” ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወመዋዒተ ፡ ፍትወት ፡ በከመ ፡ ዜነወነ ፡ አባ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እንዘ' ፡ እነብር ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አእርፍ ፤ ወአንሶሰውኩ'" ፡ በሌሊት ፡ በብርሃነ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ወርኢኩ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወሥዕርቱ ፡ ከደኖ ፡ ኩለንታሁ ። ወእቤ ፡ በልብየ ፡ እሖር ፡ እርአይ!' ፡ ምንትኑዝ ። ወሖርኩ ፡ እርአይ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየኒ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ንቅዓተ ፡ እብን ። ሶቤሃ ፡ አእመርኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወእቤሎ ፡ አስተበቀዓከ ፡ ኦብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፃእ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወእንሣእ ፡ በረከተከ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ አርመመ ፡ ወሰአልክዎ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አባ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይወፅእ ፡ ወእቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ አነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዕራቅየ ፡ ሀሎኩ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዕየ ፡ ከላእኩ ፡ ክዳንየ ፡ እንተ ፡ እትዓፀፍ ፡ ወወገርኩ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወለቢሳ ፡ ወፅአት ፡ ወጸለይነ ። ወእቤላ"፤ ፡ ንግርኒ ፡ እምየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንት ፡
[On this day also died abbä Margorëwos (= Mercurius) who (was) from the convent of abbä Päkuemis (= Pachomius). Abbä 'Entonyos (= Anthony), the superior of this monastery, said: "Monks came to me saying: 'Behold, the passing of the brother Margorëwos is near.' I went to him to receive his blessing. I found him unconscious; his soul had not separated from his body. We remained with him with the brothers three nights without us tasting food. The third day, his breath returned to him and he opened his eyes. When he saw me, he said to me: 'Why have you gathered around me?' We told him everything that concerned him and asked him to narrate to us what he had seen. He told us how a majestic man had come to him. He took his spirit and showed him the torments of the sinners and the reward of the just. Then he lamented over his soul and threw himself on the ground. || said to us: 'My brothers, go to the dwelling of abbä Giyorgis (= George) of the desert of Haräsit, for I heard the angel who (was) with me say: Send back Margorëwos and bring Giyorgis.' As for this saint Giyorgis, he was fighting (spiritually) in a small cell which is in the salt desert so much that his body cracked. When the brothers went, they found him dead, brought him to the monastery, and buried him there. As for this brother Margorëwos, he fatigued his body so much that it became like dry wood. Then he asked me for permission to go to the desert and said to me: 'The fourteenth of Nahasë, come to me, for you will find me.' The day that he had set for me, I came to him and found him. He said to me: 'I am going to die and go to my fathers. Three bulls will come to you, load my body on their backs—they will guide you to your monastery by the will of God—and bury me near my dwelling.' It was according to his word, and there manifested from his tomb miracles and wonders without number." May God have mercy through his prayer upon his dear 'Atnätéwos in the ages of ages, amen.] [On this day also died the saint 'Amata Krestos, the (spiritual) combatant, the contemner of the world, victorious over desire, as abbä Dän'ël (= Daniel) told (it) to us; he said: "While I remained in the desert to rest, I walked by night in the moonlight and I saw a man sitting on a mountain and his hair covered him entirely. I said in my heart: 'Let us go to see who (is) this one.' I went to see him. When he saw me, he entered a cleft of rock. I knew then that it was a man and I said to him: 'I beg you, man of God, to come out to me so that I may receive your blessing.' He remained silent and I begged him insistently. Then he said to me: 'Forgive me, abbä, because I will not come out.' I said to him: 'Why?' He said to me: 'Because I am a woman (and) because I am naked.' Hearing this, I took off my cloak with which I was covered and threw (it) to her. Having

ወፃእኪ ፡፣ ዝየ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ረከብኪ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ንቅዓተ ። ወትቤለኒ ፡ ድንግል ፡ አነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አበውየ ፡ ወእነብር ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዘይጌውጳኒ ፡ ዘልፈ ፡ ወይትናገር ፡ ምስሌየ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወርኢክዎ ፡እንዘ ፡ይበኪ ፡'ወእንዘ ፡ ይትአመን' ፡ ኃጢአቶ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ሶበ ፡ ጐድጐድኩ” ፡ ኢያርኃወ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አላ ፡ አንኀ ፡ ብካየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይኔስሕ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ እቤ ፡ በልብየ ፡ በእፎ ፡ አነሂ ፡ ዘኢይበኪ ፡ ወኢይኔስሕ” ፡ ኃፍረትየ ፡ ወኃሣርየ ። ወሖርኩ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡፤ ኀበ ፣ ማኅደርየ ፡ ወለበስኩ ፡ ሠቀ ። ወነሣአኩ ፡ አስፈሬዳ ፡ ዐተር ፤ ወጽዋዓ ፡ ማይ ። ወጸለይኩ ፡ ኀበ ፣ መቃብሪሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ እንዘ ፣ እብል” ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ወኃያል ፡ ወመንክር ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ዘታድኅኖሙ ፡ ለሕጉላን ፡ ወታነሥኦሙ ፡ ለውዱቃን ፡ ወትሰምዖሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይጸርተ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ፈኑ ፡ ሣሀለከ ፡ ወጽድቀከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታድኅና ፡ ለአመትከ ፡ እንተ ፡ ተወከለት ፡ ብከ ፡ ወሐውፀኒ ፡ ለአመትከ ፡ ነዳይት ፡ ወተወከፍ ፡ ንስሐየ ፡ ወባርኮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ስንቅ ፡ ወለዝንቱ ፡ ጽዋዓ ፡ መይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩነኒ ፡ ለኑኃ ፡ መዋፅልየ ፡ ከመ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ከርሥየ ፡ ኢያስትት ፡ ዘኃለይኩ ፡ በእንተ ፣፤ መድኃኒተ ፣ ነፍስየ ። ወሖርኩ ፡ ጎልጎታ ፡ ወጸለይኩ ፡ዛተ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ወወፃእኩ ፡ ወገደፍኩ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበጻሕኩ ፡ ኢያሪኮ ፡ ወሖርኩ ፡ እስከ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ኤርትራ ። ወእምህየ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወረከብኩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ንቅዓተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወእቤ ፤ ዝንቱ ፡፤ ማኅደርየ ፡ ዘአስተደለወ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወናሁ ፡ ቋወወቿዓመትየ ፡እንዘ ፡ እነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወመካን ፡ ኢርኢኩ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ዘእንበሌከ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አስፈሬዳ ፡ ወጽዋዕ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እሴሰዮሙ” ፡ ጠወቿዓመተ ፡ "ኢየኃልቁ ፡ ወኢይነትጉ” ፡ ወአልባስየሰ ፡ በልየ ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ኮነኒ ፡ ምሥዋረ ፡ ሀየንተ ፡ አልባስ ፡ ወኢያጻመወኒ” ፡ ዋዕየ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ በሐጋይ ፤ ወኢሦረ ፡ ክረምት ፡
dressed, she came out and we prayed. I said to her: 'Tell me, my mother, why you came here and how you found this cleft.' She said to me: 'I was a virgin in the house of my fathers and I remained in Jerusalem. There was a monk who always visited me and conversed with me. Some time later, I went to him and I saw him weeping and confessing his sin before God. When I knocked, he did not open to me but continued to weep while repenting. When I heard (him), I said in my heart: How do I not weep myself and do penance for my shame and my disgrace? I went quickly to my dwelling and put on a sack. I took a basket of beans and a cup of water. I prayed near the tomb of our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him!—saying: Strong, powerful, and admirable Lord forever, (you) who save the lost (beings), resurrect the fallen, and hear those who cry out to you, send your mercy and your justice so that they may save your servant who commits herself to you, visit me, (me) your miserable servant, accept my penance, and bless this viaticum and this cup of water so that they may be for me for (all) the length of my days so that, because of my belly, I do not neglect to occupy myself with the salvation of my soul. I went to Golgotä (= Golgotha), made this prayer, went out, and threw myself to God. I arrived at 'Iyäriko (= Jericho) and went as far as the Red Sea. From there, I came into this desert, found this cleft of the mountain, and said: This (is) my dwelling that God has prepared for me. Behold, it is thirty-eight years that I have remained in this desert and (this) place, and I have seen no man apart from you. As for this basket and this cup, while I have been sustained by them for thirty-eight years, they are not exhausted and have not diminished. As for my clothes, they have aged and this place has been for me a protection in place of clothes: the heat of the sun has not tormented me in summer nor the cold of winter during all my days.'"
በኩሉ ፡ መዋፅልየ። "ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አስተበቀዓተኒ” ፡ ከመ ፡ እብላዕ ፡ ወእስተይ ፡ ወበላዕኩ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ አስፈሬዳ ፣፤ ወሰተይኩ ፡፤ እምጽዋዕ ፡፣ ወኢይነትግ”' ፡ ወአእኩትክዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወፈቀድኩ ፡ እኅድግ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ክዳንየ ፡ ወዐበየት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ አምጽእ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ዘይሜኒ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወነገርክዎ ፡ ለአበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወከልሐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዘቦክልኤ ፡ ክዳናተ ፡ የሀብ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ለዘ ፡ አልቦቱ ፡ ወአምጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ። 'ወነሚእየ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ሶቤሃ” ፡ ከመ ፡ እርአያ ፡ ለአመተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወጸመውኩ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አኃሥሥ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ ወኢረከብክዋ ። ወእምድኅረ ኅዳጥ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ፪አዕሩግ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ነሐውር ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ኤርትራ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ ጣኔዎስ” ፣ ርኢነ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትነብር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ጾላእት ፡ ፍና ፡ ሰርክ ፡ ወሮፅነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንትባረክ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወጐየት ፡ ወቦአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐት ፡ 'ወቀረብነ ፡ ውስተ” ፡ አፈ ፡ በዐት ፡ ወኢርኢናሃ” ፡ ወረከብሃነ ፡ አስፈሬዳ ፡ አተር ፡ ወጽዋዓ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወበላዕነ ፡ ወኀልቀ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወቤትነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጌሴም ። ወኃሠሥናሃ ፡ ለቅድስት” ፡ ከመ ፡ ንትባረክ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወረከብናሃ ፡ በዘ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ ወጸጐራ ፡ ከደነ ፡ ሥጋሃ ። ወሰገድነ ፡
After that, she begged me to eat and drink; I ate from the basket and drank from the cup, and they did not diminish; I praised God. I wanted to leave her my cloak, but she refused, saying: 'Bring me (something) that is beautiful.' Then I went to the monastery and told (this) to the superior; he cried out saying: 'Let him who has two cloaks give one to him who has none.' They brought him what he wanted. Having taken (the cloak), I went then to see 'Amata Krestos. I took much trouble to look for her, but I did not find her. Shortly after came two old men; they said: 'While we were following the road of the Red Sea in the desert of Tänëwos (= Tanis), we saw a woman sitting near a rock at the approach of evening. We ran to be blessed by her, but she fled and entered a cave. We approached the entrance of the cave but did not see her. We found a basket of beans and a cup of water. We ate and (the beans) were then finished. We spent the night (in that place) until the next day. We looked for the saint to be blessed by her; we found her dead, her hair covered her body. We prostrated ourselves, buried her in the cave, obstructed the entrance of it with a stone, and we


[p. 344] {#po.47.p344}
ወቀበርናሃ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐት ፡ ወደፈነ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ በእብን ፡ ወአተውነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ንባርኮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ረሰየነ ፣፤ ድልዋነ ፡ ንርከብ ፡ ወንንሣእ” ፡ በረከታ ፡ ለቅድስት ። ወሰሚዕፅየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አዕመርኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እንተ ፡ ረከብክዋ ። ወነገርክዎሙ ፡ ዘተናገረተኒ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይረድኦ ፡ ለንዋይ ፡ ድኩም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይማኦ ፡ ለጸላኢ ፡ ወለኩሉ ፡ አጋንንቲሁ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡
returned, blessing God because he had made us worthy to find and receive the blessing of the saint. Hearing this, I knew that it was the one I had met. I told them what she had said to me and they glorified God who helps a weak instrument to defeat the Enemy and all his demons." May God have mercy through her prayer upon his dear 'Atnätéwos in the ages of ages, amen.]
በጸሎታ ፡ ለፍቁራ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ክቡር ፡ አባ ፡ ኤኢፋንዮስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቆብሮስ” ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፤ አመ፲ወኒለግንቦት”" ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቆብሮስ” ፡ በከመ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ አፈ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢይበጽሕ ፡ ኅበ?” ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ጾርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወአብጽሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቆብሮስቾ ፡ ወወፅኡ” ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ በወንጌላት ፡ ወበመስቀላት ፡ ወበማኅትው ፡ ወማዕጠንታት ፡ ወፅጣን” ። ወጾሩ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ በዝማሬ ፡ ወበማኅሌታት ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ እስከ ፡ አንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወፈቀዱ ፡ ካህናት! ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክርዩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መቃብረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንተ ፡ ተዓቢ ፤ ጉባዒት” ። ወተቀረንዎሙ ፡ ፪ዲያቆናት ፡ እኩያን ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ አውገዞሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እከየ ፡ ምግባሮሙ ፡ ወከልእዎሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክረዩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መቃብረ ። ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ፪መዓልተ ፡ ወኢተወለጠ ፡ ጹናሁ” ፡ አላ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይነውም ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ፳ዲያቆን ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አነ ፡ አአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ብከ ፡ ሞገሰ ፡ በኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአንተ ፡ ከሀሊ ፡ ትመውኦሙ ፡ ለመስተቃርናን" ፡ እኩያን ። ወእምዝ ፡ አኃዘ ፤ መክሪተ ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ ወዘበጠ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ምድረ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ናሁ” ፡ እሉ ፡ ፪ዲያቆናት ፡ እኩያን ፡ ወድቁ ፡ በገጾሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወአልጸቁ ፡ ለመዊት ፡ ወጾርዎሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ ወሞቱ ፡ በሣልስት ፡ ዕለት ። ወሥጋሁሰ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ገነዝዎ ፡ ካህናት ፡ በዕፍረታት ፡፤ ብዙኃት ፡፣ ወጠብለልዎ ፡ በአልባሳት ፡ ሠናያት" ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሣጹን ፡ ዘእብን ፡ ክቡር" ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ቀበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ዓቢያት ፡ በከመ ፡ኮነ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ዘይገብር” ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ፅረፍቱ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡
On this day, the body of the saint (and) glorious abbä 'Épifänyos (= Epiphanius) arrived in the city of Qobros (= Cyprus). This (took place) thus: when the saint died on a boat on the seventeenth of Genbot before arriving at the city of Qobros, according to what the saint Yohannes Mouth of Gold (= John Chrysostom) had prophesied about him—namely that he would not arrive at his (episcopal) seat—when (therefore) he died on the way, they transported him by boat and brought him to the city of Qobros. The priests and all the people went out with the Gospels, with crosses, and with candles, censers, and incense. They carried his pure body with canticles and numerous songs until they had placed it in the church. The priests wanted to dig for him a tomb in the great church, the Catholic. Two evil deacons whom the saint had formerly excommunicated because of the vileness of their actions opposed them and prevented those who wanted to dig a tomb for him (from doing so). (The body) remained in the church four days. Its odor did not change, but he was like (someone) who sleeps. A holy deacon stood up and said to the body of the saint: "I know that you have grace before God, you powerful one, you will defeat the wicked adversaries." Then he took a pickaxe in his hands and struck the earth with it. Behold, immediately the two evil deacons fell to the ground on their faces and were near to dying. They were carried to their houses and they died the third day. As for the body of the saint, the priests shrouded it with numerous perfumes, wrapped it in beautiful clothes, and placed it in a sarcophagus of precious stone. Then they buried it in the church. There appeared from his body great miracles as (the saint) had done before, before his death. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአባ ፡ 'ስንጣ ፡ ወአጋቦስ”' ፡ ሰማዕት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day (also), there is the commemoration of abbä Sentä and (of) 'Agäbos the martyr. May God have mercy through their prayer upon their dear 'Atnätéwos in the ages of ages, amen.]


[p. 346] {#po.47.p346}

### READING FOR THE 29TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ፳8ለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቀዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ በዓለ ፤ ልደቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወአምላክነ ፡ ወመድኃኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት'፤ ፡ ወክብር" ፡ ወሰጊድ! ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ር ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ዘእምደብረ" ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ማርታ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ 'ምግባራት ፣፤ መንክራት" ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ትፅንስ ፡ እሙ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ፡ በንዋም ፡ ወዜነዋ ፡ በልደቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ' ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወአብአ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ታሕተ ፡፤ አርዑተ ፤ ምንኩስና ፤ ወተጸምደ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወአስተርአዩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መላእክት ፡ በንዋም ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ ለያልይ ፡ ወመሀርዎ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ በከመ ፡ አስተርአዩ"' ፡ ለአባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ። ወከሠቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ምግባረ"! ፡ ዘኢያማስን ፡ ገድለ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወዘያጸንዓ! ። ወተጋደለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዘይትሌዓል ፡ ወዘይፈደፍድ ፡ እምእለ ፡ ይለብሱ ፡ ሥጋ ። እስመ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ያመጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ መንፈሳዌ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወእምብገኃ ፡ ተጋድሎቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡፪ሄዓመተ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ቿዓመተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወነበረ!" ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፡ ዓውደ" ፡ አእባን ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ሎቱ ፡ ወኢወፅአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ፳ዓመት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ጓቋወ፳ዓመተ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ጅወ፳ዓመተ ። በውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ፄዓመተ ፡ ወበውስተ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ምንኩስና ፤ ድዘዓመተ ፡ ወተኣምራቲሁሰ”"' ፡ መኑ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ተናግሮቶሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ በገድሉ፤' ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ተኣምራት” ። ወደረሰ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፤ ድርሳናተ ፣፤ ብዙኃተ ፤ ወተግሣጸጻተ ፤ ወቃላተ ፤ በሞዔተ ፡ ለምንኩስና ፡ ወለመድኃኒተ ፡ ነፍስ ። ወተርጐመ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ እመጸሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, (there is) the commemoration of the feast of the birth of our Lord, our God and our Savior Jesus Christ—to him glory, honor, and adoration with his excellent Father and the Holy Spirit in the ages of ages, amen! On this day also died the holy father abbä Sem'on (= Simon) who (was) from the mountain of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch). The name of the father of this saint was Yohannes (= John) and the name of his mother, Märtä (= Martha). Prodigious actions occurred because of him. This (took place) thus: before his mother conceived, the saint Yohannes (= John) the Baptist came to her during her sleep, announced to her the birth of this saint, and revealed to her what would become of him. When he was born and (his) days were six years, he went to the mountain of 'Ansokiyä, put himself under the yoke of monastic life, devoted himself to asceticism, and led a great (spiritual) combat. Angels appeared to him during his sleep many nights—and taught him the monastic life—as they had appeared to abbä Päkuemis (= Pachomius). They revealed to this saint the way of acting that does not corrupt the monastic life and that strengthens it. This saint led a (spiritual) combat that exceeded and surpassed (the strength of) those who are clothed with flesh. The angels brought him spiritual food all the time. By the greatness of his (spiritual) combat, he mounted a column and stood there six years. Then he passed to another column, large, and stood there eight years. Then he passed into the mountain and remained there twenty years inside an enclosure of stones that he had made for himself. He did not go out of it until the expiration of the twenty years. Then he mounted to the summit of a large column and stood there forty-five years. All the days of his life were eighty-five years: in the house of his father, six years, and in the monastic combat, seventy-nine years. As for his miracles, who can recount them? In (the history of) his life, certainly, numerous miracles (are) written. This father composed numerous homilies and useful exhortations and discourses for the monastic life and the salvation of the soul. He translated numerous books of the Church and died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us, amen.
አሜን። 1ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ይትገበር ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አፍጹ ፡ ወለአባ ፡ ጉባ ፡ ዘእም፪ቅዱሳን ፡ እንግልጋ ፡ ወለይስሐቅ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ወዕረፍተ ፡ እስክንድር ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፊልይስ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also is made the commemoration of abbä 'Afsë and of abbä Gubä who (are) among the nine saints, (of) 'Engelgä and of Yeshaq (= Isaac) the monk. And (there is) the death of 'Eskender (= Alexander), the king son of Filpos (= Philip). May God have mercy through their prayer upon their dear 'Atnätéwos in the ages of ages, amen.]


[p. 348] {#po.47.p348}

### READING FOR THE 30TH OF GENBOT
አመ ፡ ቋለግንቦት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ' ፡ አባ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፳ወቷ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ኮነ ፡ ማእምረ ፡ ወተምህረ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መጸሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እምንአሱ ፡ ወአጽንዓ ፡ በልቡ ፤ ብዙኃነ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ። ወፈተወት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትጋደል ፤ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ" ፡ ምንኩስና ፣ መለኮታዊት ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ሐራ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔረ ፡ እምንእሱ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወሐደረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ወተሠይመ ፡ ቀሲሰ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፤ ወፅአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰንጋር" ፡ ዘበምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ሞቅሐ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዖማዕት ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፳ዓመት ፡ ወዘይፈደፍድ ። ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡፤ በዓት ፡ ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ወትሩፋቲሁ ፡ ወጽድቁ ፡ ወአእምሮቱ ፡ ወተሰናአዉ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፣ ወልሂቃን፤' ፡ ወአኃዝዎ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወሖረ ፡ በሚመተ” ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስና ፡ ሑረተ ፡ ሠናይት ፡ በንጽሕና ፡ ወመነነ" ፡ ጥሪተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኢየጥረየ ፡ 8ድርጊመ ፡ ወኢአሐደ ፡ ዲናረ ፡ ወዘኮነ'' ፡ ያመጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እምጸባሕት ፡ ዘይደልዎ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይሴሰይ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወዘተረፈ!! ፡ ይሁቦሙ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን' ፡ ወለፅኑሳን ። ወገብረ ፡ ንዋያተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ለአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት” ፡ ወለመጸሕፍት” ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ያነብብ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ላዕለ ፤ ሕዝብ ፤ ወይጌሥጾሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ እመጻሕፍት ፡ ወአሐደ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ እምአፉሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ሑረቶ ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፎ ፡ እምጻማ ፡፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስቡሕ” ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወአምጽአ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሕማመ ፡ 'አሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ወአሐተ" ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወኢተናገረ ፡ ግሙራ ። ወበጊዜ ፡ ፅረፍቱ ፡ ሰብሖ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወዓተበ ፡ ርእሶ'' ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father abbä Mikä'ël (= Michael), patriarch of Alexandria; among the patriarch fathers, he is the sixty-eighth. This father was learned; he had learned all the books of the Church from his childhood and had retained by heart many of them. His pure soul desired that he fight (spiritually) in the divine monastic way and become a soldier for God from his childhood. He went to the desert of 'Asgêtes (= Scetis), remained at the monastery of the holy abbä Magäres (= Macarius) many years, and was ordained priest. After that, he left for the city of Sangär (= Singar) which (is) in the land of Egypt. He shut himself in a cell for twenty years and more and led a great (spiritual) combat in this cell. His history, his virtues, his justice, and his knowledge spread and the bishops and the elders (of the people) agreed, seized him, and named him patriarch of the city of Alexandria. In his patriarchal office, he led a beautiful conduct in purity; he despised the goods of this world and did not possess a single gold coin nor a single silver coin. What was brought to him as tax that belonged to him, he fed himself on a small part of it and gave the rest to the poor, the needy, and the destitute. He had (made) from it sacred vessels for the churches and numerous books. He constantly read the Scriptures to the people and exhorted them every day, once from the Scriptures, once from his own resources. When he had finished his beautiful career, God wanted to make him rest from the pains of this world so that he might receive the glorious crown in the kingdom of heaven. God brought upon him an illness for a day and a night, and he spoke no more. At the moment of his death, he glorified Christ, made on himself the sign of the precious cross, and committed his soul into the hand of God to whom (belongs) the glory forever. All the days of his office were nine years (and) eight months. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
ለዓለም ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ 8ዓመት ፡ ፳አወራኅ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ቆሮስ ፡ ዘእምድወ፪አርድእት”፤ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ሐዋርያ” ፡ ተለአኮ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ዓመት ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፅርገቱ ፤ ለእግዚእነ ፥ ተለአኮሙ ፣ ለሐዋርያት” ፣፡ ወመልአ ፡ እምጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተለአኮ ፡ ለጳውሎስ” ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወጾረ ፡ መልእክታቲሁ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ እምነ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወአሕዛብ ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ምሥራቀ” ፡ በሐውርት ፡ ወምዕራቢሆሙ” ፡ ወበጽሖ” ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወሐዘን ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወአዕረፈ” ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also died the apostle Qoros (= Cyprus) who (is) of the seventy-two disciples. This apostle served our Lord Jesus Christ for three years. After the ascension of our Lord, he served the Apostles and was filled with the grace of the Holy Spirit. Then he served Pawlos (= Paul) the Apostle and carried his letters into numerous cities. He taught many Jews and Gentiles and baptized them with Christian baptism. He went into the east and into the west of the countries, great misfortunes and sorrows happened to him, and he died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us, amen.


[p. 350] {#po.47.p350}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ካፅበ ፤ አእረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ አርዋ ፡ ተጋዳሊት ፡ ወመዋዒተ ፡ ፍትወት ፡ ዘአሥመረቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር” ፡ በምግባራ ፡ ሠናይ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁራ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቅርታ ፡ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also died the saint 'Arwä, the (spiritual) combatant victorious over desire who pleased God by her beautiful actions. May her prayer and her blessing be with her dear 'Atnätéwos and with her dear Kotolikäwit in the ages of ages, amen.]

[On this day died the saint 'Arwä, the (spiritual) combatant victorious over desire, the Israelite. M (Narrative) that 'Amr son of Hasën the naked told; the father of all the children of the priest (= 'Amr?) told it to Yesmä'ël (= Ishmael), Yesmä'ël told it to Malfal and (the latter?) told it (There was) a man among (the people of) Israel who had many goods and had a wife by the name of 'Arwä. (She was) pretty and beautiful and there was no one like her in those days. Her husband went on a trip to a distant land. He transmitted to his brother Yä'qob (= Jacob) all the administration of his house. His brother went in to 'Arwä and said to her: "I have remained loving you twenty years, but I feared your husband; now I love you (still), and now your husband has gone far away and I want to sleep with you." 'Arwä said to him: "If my husband has gone far away, is God far? If my husband does not see me, God sees me from heaven." He left her and a demon met him—have you (ever) seen (someone) who (was) like him, the sweet word, the radiant beauty? "Let me, and I will do for you everything you want." And he returned to her and said to her: "If you sleep with me, I will act well (toward you). If you refuse me (it), I will bring upon you a misfortune." 'Arwä said to him: "What will you bring against me?" The man said to her: "I will produce against you a witness from among the greats of Israel." She said to him: "About what will you produce a witness against me?" He said to her: "Because you have married another man." 'Arwä said to him: "I will not sleep with you, for you are the brother of my husband; say what you want, God will save me from your hand." When she had refused him (it), he went, the next day, to the governor and to the people. All gathered and he said to them: "My brother's wife has married another man while her husband was alive where he was traveling." They made 'Arwä appear, they produced against her four witnesses and they said to these: "Have you seen her sleeping with a man?" The four lying witnesses said: "We have seen her sleeping with a man." The governor ordered her to be taken outside and they dragged her by the neck, by her garment. The governor, with the people, stoned her; the blood flowed from her face, she died, and they buried her because of the radiance of her beauty. As for the children of Israel, previously they did not bury a corpse if they (had) stoned it, but her, they buried. An Egyptian happened by that road, traveling and having loaded (with merchandise) five camels. He heard a voice in the tomb, he went to see the tomb, and found in the tomb 'Arwä lying, her face shining
